"""Signing in and out, who am I, changing my password, and what's waiting on me."""

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Annotated

import typer

from mission_control.cli.client import ApiError
from mission_control.cli.commands.common import Json, command, start
from mission_control.cli.config import Credentials
from mission_control.cli.prompts import ask, ask_new_secret, ask_secret
from mission_control.cli.refusals import refusals
from mission_control.cli.render import ROLE, Terminal, plural
from mission_control.cli.views import identity, org_name


def register(app: typer.Typer) -> None:
    command(app, "login", example="mc login --email lee@aurora.example")(login)
    command(app, "logout", example="mc logout")(logout)
    command(app, "whoami", example="mc whoami")(whoami)
    command(app, "password", example="mc password")(password)
    command(app, "inbox", example="mc inbox")(inbox)


def login(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    email: Annotated[
        str | None,
        typer.Option(
            "--email", "-e", metavar="EMAIL", help="Your email address (asked if left out)"
        ),
    ] = None,
) -> None:
    """Log in. Your password is asked for with the input hidden.

    Without a terminal, pass --email and pipe the password on stdin.
    """
    env = start(ctx)
    if email is None:
        email = ask(
            env,
            "Email",
            missing="No email given, and there's no terminal to ask on. Pass --email, and pipe "
            "the password on stdin.",
        )
    secret = ask_secret(env, "Password")
    with refusals():
        session = env.anonymous_api().post("/auth/login", {"email": email, "password": secret})
    env.store.save(Credentials(session["token"], email, session.get("expires_at")))
    me = env.api().get("/me")

    about = ROLE.get(me["role"], me["role"])
    if org := org_name(me):
        about = f"{about}, {org}"
    lasts = _session_length(session.get("expires_at"))
    env.term.ok(f"Logged in as {me['name']} ({about}).{lasts}")
    env.term.next_steps(["mc inbox"])


def logout(ctx: typer.Context) -> None:
    """Log out and end this session on the server."""
    env = start(ctx)
    if env.store.load() is None:
        env.term.ok("You're not logged in.")
        return
    try:
        env.api().post("/auth/logout")
    except ApiError as error:
        if error.status != 401:  # a session that already ended is as good as logged out
            raise
    env.store.clear()
    env.term.ok("Logged out.")


def whoami(ctx: typer.Context, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Show who you're logged in as."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    me = env.api().get("/me")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(me)
        return
    env.term.fields(
        identity(me) + [("Crew profile", "yes" if me.get("crew_profile_id") else "none")]
    )


def password(ctx: typer.Context) -> None:
    """Change your password. Asks for your current one, then the new one twice.

    Every session ends afterwards, including this one, so log in again.
    """
    env = start(ctx)
    api = env.api()
    current = ask_secret(env, "Current password")
    new = ask_new_secret(
        env,
        "New password",
        "Repeat new password",
        mismatch="The new passwords don't match. Nothing was changed.",
    )
    api.post("/me/password", {"current_password": current, "new_password": new})
    env.store.clear()
    env.term.ok("Password changed. All your sessions have ended, including this one.")
    env.term.next_steps(["mc login"])


def inbox(ctx: typer.Context, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Show what's waiting on you.

    Proposals to answer, missions waiting for your approval, and your missions.
    """
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    data = env.api().get("/me/inbox")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(data)
        return
    term = env.term
    proposals = data.get("proposals") or []
    approvals = data.get("approvals") or []
    missions = data.get("missions") or []
    if not (proposals or approvals or missions):
        term.ok("Nothing needs your attention.")
        return

    sections = 0
    if proposals:
        sections = _section(term, sections, "Proposals waiting for your answer")
        term.table(
            ["Assignment", "Mission", "Slot", "Dates"],
            [
                [
                    str(a["assignment_id"]),
                    a["mission_name"],
                    a["slot_name"],
                    term.dates(a["start_date"], a["end_date"]),
                ]
                for a in proposals
            ],
        )
    if approvals:
        sections = _section(term, sections, "Missions waiting for your approval")
        term.table(
            ["ID", "Mission", "Dates", "Approvals"],
            [
                [
                    str(m["mission_id"]),
                    m["mission_name"],
                    term.dates(m["start_date"], m["end_date"]),
                    f"{m['approvals']} of {m['required']}",
                ]
                for m in approvals
            ],
        )
    if missions:
        sections = _section(term, sections, "Your draft missions")
        term.table(
            ["ID", "Mission", "Dates", "Waiting on"],
            [
                [
                    str(m["mission_id"]),
                    m["mission_name"],
                    term.dates(m["start_date"], m["end_date"]),
                    _waiting_on(m),
                ]
                for m in missions
            ],
        )

    steps = []
    if proposals:
        first = proposals[0]["assignment_id"]
        steps += [f"mc assignments accept {first}", f"mc assignments decline {first}"]
    if approvals:
        steps.append(f"mc missions approve {approvals[0]['mission_id']}")
    ready = [m for m in missions if m["ready"]]
    unfilled = [m for m in missions if m["unfilled"]]
    if ready:
        steps.append(f"mc missions submit {ready[0]['mission_id']}")
    elif unfilled:
        steps.append(f"mc missions match {unfilled[0]['mission_id']}")
    term.next_steps(steps)


def _waiting_on(mission: dict) -> str:
    """What one of your draft missions needs before it can be submitted."""
    if mission["ready"]:
        return "Ready to submit"
    parts = [
        f"{s['slot_name']}: {plural(s['open_places'], 'place')} unfilled"
        for s in mission["unfilled"]
    ]
    if mission["unanswered"]:
        parts.append(f"{plural(mission['unanswered'], 'reply', 'replies')} awaited")
    return "; ".join(parts)


def _section(term: Terminal, shown: int, title: str) -> int:
    if shown:
        term.line()
    term.line(title)
    return shown + 1


def _session_length(expires_at: str | None) -> str:
    if not expires_at:
        return ""
    try:
        expires = datetime.fromisoformat(expires_at.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    except ValueError:
        return ""
    hours = round((expires - datetime.now(UTC)).total_seconds() / 3600)
    return f" Session lasts {plural(hours, 'hour')}." if hours >= 1 else ""
"""Running the organisation (Directors): users and roles, the skill catalogue, and how many
approvals a mission needs."""

from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Annotated

import typer

from mission_control.cli.commands.common import Email, Json, command, group, start
from mission_control.cli.prompts import ask_new_secret
from mission_control.cli.render import ROLE, plural
from mission_control.cli.resolve import skill_named, user_by_email

users_app = group("Users and roles (Directors).")
skills_app = group("The skill catalogue (Directors manage it; Leads can list it).")
org_app = group("Organisation settings (Directors).")


class RoleName(StrEnum):
    director = "director"
    lead = "lead"
    crew = "crew"


RoleArg = Annotated[RoleName, typer.Argument(metavar="ROLE", help="director, lead or crew")]
SkillName = Annotated[str, typer.Argument(metavar="NAME", help="The skill's name")]


def _a(role: str) -> str:
    label = ROLE.get(role, role)
    return f"an {label}" if label[0] in "AEIOU" else f"a {label}"


# Users


@command(users_app, "list", example="mc users list")
def list_users(ctx: typer.Context, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """List everyone in your organisation."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    users = env.api().get("/users")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(users)
        return
    env.term.table(
        ["ID", "Name", "Email", "Role", "Status"],
        [
            [
                str(u["id"]),
                u["name"],
                u["email"],
                ROLE.get(u["role"], u["role"]),
                "active" if u["active"] else "deactivated",
            ]
            for u in users
        ],
    )


@command(
    users_app,
    "add",
    example='mc users add --email zoe@aurora.example --name "Zoe Lin" --role crew',
)
def add_user(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    email: Annotated[
        str, typer.Option("--email", metavar="EMAIL", help="Their email; they log in with it")
    ],
    name: Annotated[str, typer.Option("--name", metavar="NAME", help="Their full name")],
    role: Annotated[
        RoleName, typer.Option("--role", metavar="ROLE", help="director, lead or crew")
    ],
    json_output: Json = False,
) -> None:
    """Add a user. You set their initial password, asked for twice with the input hidden."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    secret = ask_new_secret(
        env,
        "Initial password",
        "Repeat initial password",
        mismatch="The passwords don't match. Nothing was changed.",
    )
    user = api.post(
        "/users", {"email": email, "name": name, "role": role.value, "password": secret}
    )
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(user)
        return
    env.term.ok(f"Added {user['name']} ({user['email']}) as {_a(user['role'])}.")
    env.term.next_steps([f"They log in with: mc login --email {user['email']}"])


@command(users_app, "set-role", example="mc users set-role nora@aurora.example director")
def set_role(ctx: typer.Context, email: Email, role: RoleArg, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Change someone's role."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    user = user_by_email(api, email)
    updated = api.patch(f"/users/{user['id']}", {"role": role.value})
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(updated)
        return
    env.term.ok(f"{user['name']} is now {_a(role.value)}.")


@command(users_app, "deactivate", example="mc users deactivate chen@aurora.example")
def deactivate(ctx: typer.Context, email: Email, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Deactivate someone. They can't log in, and their history stays."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    user = user_by_email(api, email)
    updated = api.patch(f"/users/{user['id']}", {"active": False})
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(updated)
        return
    env.term.ok(f"Deactivated {user['name']}. They can no longer log in.")


@command(users_app, "enable-crew", example="mc users enable-crew lee@aurora.example")
def enable_crew(ctx: typer.Context, email: Email, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Give someone a crew profile, so they can be put on missions."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    user = user_by_email(api, email)
    profile = api.post(f"/users/{user['id']}/crew-profile")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(profile)
        return
    env.term.ok(f"{user['name']} now has a crew profile.")


# Skills


@command(skills_app, "list", example="mc skills list")
def list_skills(ctx: typer.Context, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """List the skill catalogue."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    skills = env.api().get("/skills")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(skills)
        return
    if not skills:
        env.term.line("No skills yet.")
        return
    if any(s.get("category") for s in skills):
        env.term.table(
            ["Skill", "Category"], [[s["name"], s.get("category") or ""] for s in skills]
        )
    else:
        env.term.lines(s["name"] for s in skills)


@command(skills_app, "add", example='mc skills add "Robotics"')
def add_skill(ctx: typer.Context, name: SkillName, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Add a skill to the catalogue."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    skill = env.api().post("/skills", {"name": name})
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(skill)
        return
    env.term.ok(f"Added skill {skill['name']}.")


@command(skills_app, "rename", example='mc skills rename Geology "Planetary Geology"')
def rename_skill(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    name: SkillName,
    new_name: Annotated[str, typer.Argument(metavar="NEW_NAME", help="What to call it")],
    json_output: Json = False,
) -> None:
    """Rename a skill. Everyone who has it keeps it."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    skill = skill_named(api, name)
    renamed = api.patch(f"/skills/{skill['id']}", {"name": new_name})
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(renamed)
        return
    env.term.ok(f"Renamed {skill['name']} to {renamed['name']}.")


@command(skills_app, "remove", example="mc skills remove Robotics")
def remove_skill(ctx: typer.Context, name: SkillName) -> None:
    """Remove a skill nobody has and no slot requires."""
    env = start(ctx)
    api = env.api()
    skill = skill_named(api, name)
    api.delete(f"/skills/{skill['id']}")
    env.term.ok(f"Removed skill {skill['name']}.")


# Organisation


@command(org_app, "show", example="mc org show")
def show_org(ctx: typer.Context, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Show your organisation's settings."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    org = env.api().get("/org")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(org)
        return
    env.term.fields(
        [("Organisation", org["name"]), ("Approvals required", str(org["required_approvals"]))]
    )


@command(org_app, "set-approvals", example="mc org set-approvals 2")
def set_approvals(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    count: Annotated[
        int, typer.Argument(metavar="N", help="Approvals a mission needs to go active")
    ],
    json_output: Json = False,
) -> None:
    """Set how many Directors must approve a mission."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    org = env.api().patch("/org", {"required_approvals": count})
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(org)
        return
    env.term.ok(
        f"Missions now need {plural(org['required_approvals'], 'approval')}. Missions already "
        "pending keep the number they were submitted with."
    )
"""Crew profiles: list and view them, see someone's assignment history, and set skill levels."""

from typing import Annotated

import typer

from mission_control.cli.commands.common import Email, Json, command, group, start
from mission_control.cli.refusals import refusals
from mission_control.cli.resolve import crew_by_email, skill_named
from mission_control.cli.views import assignment_summary_rows, show_profile, skills_text

app = group("Crew profiles, skill levels and assignment history.")

Skill = Annotated[str, typer.Argument(metavar="SKILL", help="The skill's name")]


@command(app, "list", example="mc crew list")
def list_crew(ctx: typer.Context, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """List the crew you can see."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    crew = env.api().get("/crew")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(crew)
        return
    if not crew:
        env.term.line("No crew to show.")
        return
    env.term.table(
        ["Name", "Email", "Skills"],
        [
            [
                c["name"] + ("" if c.get("active", True) else " (deactivated)"),
                c["email"],
                skills_text(c.get("skills") or []),
            ]
            for c in crew
        ],
    )


@command(app, "show", example="mc crew show fay@aurora.example")
def show(ctx: typer.Context, email: Email, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Show someone's profile, skills and unavailable dates."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    person = crew_by_email(api, email)
    profile = api.get(f"/crew/{person['crew_profile_id']}")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(profile)
        return
    pairs = [("Name", profile["name"]), ("Email", profile["email"])]
    if not profile.get("active", True):
        pairs.append(("Status", "deactivated"))
    show_profile(env.term, pairs, profile, own=False)


@command(app, "history", example="mc crew history chen@aurora.example")
def history(ctx: typer.Context, email: Email, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Show someone's assignment history, including missions they've left."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    person = crew_by_email(api, email)
    rows = api.get(f"/crew/{person['crew_profile_id']}/assignments")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(rows)
        return
    if not rows:
        env.term.line(f"{person['name']} has no assignments yet.")
        return
    env.term.table(
        ["Assignment", "Mission", "Slot", "Dates", "Status"],
        assignment_summary_rows(env.term, rows),
    )


@command(app, "set-skill", example="mc crew set-skill ana@aurora.example Piloting 5")
def set_skill(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    email: Email,
    skill: Skill,
    level: Annotated[int, typer.Argument(metavar="LEVEL", help="1 to 5")],
    json_output: Json = False,
) -> None:
    """Set someone's level in a skill. Leads and Directors, never for themselves."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    person = crew_by_email(api, email)
    found = skill_named(api, skill)
    with refusals():
        result = api.put(
            f"/crew/{person['crew_profile_id']}/skills/{found['id']}", {"level": level}
        )
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(result)
        return
    env.term.ok(f"Set {person['name']}'s {found['name']} to {level}.")


@command(app, "unset-skill", example="mc crew unset-skill ana@aurora.example Leadership")
def unset_skill(ctx: typer.Context, email: Email, skill: Skill) -> None:
    """Remove a skill from someone's profile. Leads and Directors, never for themselves."""
    env = start(ctx)
    api = env.api()
    person = crew_by_email(api, email)
    found = skill_named(api, skill)
    api.delete(f"/crew/{person['crew_profile_id']}/skills/{found['id']}")
    env.term.ok(f"Removed {found['name']} from {person['name']}'s skills.")

"""The CLI's only way into Mission Control: JSON requests to the HTTP API through an `httpx2`
client. Tests pass in a client with a mock transport, or the API's own test client, so no server
is needed."""

from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import httpx2

from mission_control.cli.errors import AUTHENTICATION, REFUSED, UNAVAILABLE

API_PREFIX = "/api/v1"
TIMEOUT = httpx2.Timeout(15.0, connect=5.0)  # the server may wait up to 5 s for a busy database


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Problem:
    code: str
    message: str
    refs: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


class ApiError(Exception):
    """The server refused the request. Carries its error body: a code, a message and every
    problem. Commands may attach a `subject` (e.g. "Mission 5"), whether a state refusal should
    name it, and hints for known codes, which are used when the refusal is shown."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        status: int,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        problems: tuple[Problem, ...] = (),
        body: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.status = status
        self.code = code
        self.message = message
        self.problems = problems
        self.body: Mapping[str, Any] = body or {}
        self.subject: str | None = None
        self.name_state = False
        self.hints: dict[str, str] = {}

    @property
    def exit_code(self) -> int:
        if self.status == 401:
            return AUTHENTICATION
        if self.status == 503:
            return UNAVAILABLE
        return REFUSED

    @property
    def has_error_body(self) -> bool:
        return isinstance(self.body.get("error"), Mapping)


class Unreachable(Exception):
    def __init__(self, url: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(url)
        self.url = url


class Api:
    """Sends requests with the session token and turns error responses into `ApiError`.

    A 401 on a request that carried a token means the session is over, so
    `on_session_ended` runs (the CLI clears the saved token) before the error is raised."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        http: httpx2.Client,
        token: str | None = None,
        on_session_ended: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._http = http
        self._token = token
        self._on_session_ended = on_session_ended

    def get(self, path: str, params: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> Any:
        return self._send("GET", path, params=params)

    def post(self, path: str, body: Any = None) -> Any:
        return self._send("POST", path, body=body)

    def put(self, path: str, body: Any = None) -> Any:
        return self._send("PUT", path, body=body)

    def patch(self, path: str, body: Any = None) -> Any:
        return self._send("PATCH", path, body=body)

    def delete(self, path: str) -> Any:
        return self._send("DELETE", path)

    def _send(
        self,
        method: str,
        path: str,
        *,
        body: Any = None,
        params: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
    ) -> Any:
        headers = {"Accept": "application/json"}
        if self._token:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self._token}"
        try:
            response = self._http.request(
                method,
                API_PREFIX + path,
                json=body,
                params=dict(params) if params else None,
                headers=headers,
            )
        except httpx2.TransportError as exc:
            raise Unreachable(str(self._http.base_url).rstrip("/")) from exc

        if response.is_success:
            if response.status_code == 204 or not response.content:
                return None
            try:
                return response.json()
            except ValueError:
                raise _unexpected(response) from None

        failure = _error(response)
        if response.status_code == 401 and self._token and self._on_session_ended:
            self._on_session_ended()
        raise failure


def _error(response: httpx2.Response) -> ApiError:
    try:
        body = response.json()
    except ValueError:
        return _unexpected(response)
    error = body.get("error") if isinstance(body, dict) else None
    if not (
        isinstance(error, dict)
        and isinstance(error.get("code"), str)
        and isinstance(error.get("message"), str)
    ):
        return _unexpected(response)
    problems = tuple(
        Problem(str(p.get("code", "")), str(p.get("message", "")), p.get("refs") or {})
        for p in error.get("problems") or ()
        if isinstance(p, dict)
    )
    return ApiError(response.status_code, error["code"], error["message"], problems, body)


def _unexpected(response: httpx2.Response) -> ApiError:
    return ApiError(
        response.status_code,
        "unexpected_response",
        f"The server sent an unexpected response (HTTP {response.status_code})",
    )
"""Where the CLI finds the server, and where it keeps the session from `mc login`: a small JSON
file that only the user can read."""

import json
import os
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from pathlib import Path

DEFAULT_API_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8000"
_CREDENTIALS_FILE = "credentials.json"


def default_config_dir() -> Path:
    return Path.home() / ".config" / "mission-control"


def api_url() -> str:
    """The server's base URL. `MC_API_URL` overrides the local default."""
    return (os.environ.get("MC_API_URL") or "").strip().rstrip("/") or DEFAULT_API_URL


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Credentials:
    token: str
    email: str
    expires_at: str | None = None


class CredentialStore:
    """The session token on disk. The file is written with mode 0600 inside a 0700 directory,
    and replaced atomically so a crash never leaves half a file."""

    def __init__(self, directory: Path) -> None:
        self.directory = directory

    @property
    def path(self) -> Path:
        return self.directory / _CREDENTIALS_FILE

    def load(self) -> Credentials | None:
        """The saved session, or None when there isn't a usable one."""
        try:
            data = json.loads(self.path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            return None
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            return None
        token = data.get("token")
        if not isinstance(token, str) or not token:
            return None
        expires_at = data.get("expires_at")
        return Credentials(
            token=token,
            email=str(data.get("email") or ""),
            expires_at=expires_at if isinstance(expires_at, str) else None,
        )

    def save(self, credentials: Credentials) -> None:
        self.directory.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        os.chmod(self.directory, 0o700)
        temporary = self.path.with_name(self.path.name + ".tmp")
        fd = os.open(temporary, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)
        try:
            os.fchmod(fd, 0o600)  # the mode given to open() is ignored if the file existed
            with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as file:
                json.dump(asdict(credentials), file)
        except BaseException:
            temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            raise
        os.replace(temporary, self.path)

    def clear(self) -> None:
        self.path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
"""What every command runs with: the HTTP client, the saved session, the terminal and whether
there's anyone at the keyboard. Tests build one with a mock client and a temporary config
directory; a real run builds the defaults."""

import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

import httpx2

from mission_control.cli.client import TIMEOUT, Api
from mission_control.cli.config import CredentialStore, api_url, default_config_dir
from mission_control.cli.errors import NotLoggedIn
from mission_control.cli.prompts import Prompter, TerminalPrompter
from mission_control.cli.render import Terminal


@dataclass
class Env:
    http: httpx2.Client | None = None
    config_dir: Path | None = None
    interactive: bool | None = None  # None: ask whether stdin is a terminal
    color: bool | None = None  # None: decide from the terminal and NO_COLOR
    prompter: Prompter = field(default_factory=TerminalPrompter)
    json_output: bool = False  # set by a command run with --json
    _terminal: Terminal | None = field(default=None, init=False, repr=False)

    @property
    def store(self) -> CredentialStore:
        return CredentialStore(self.config_dir or default_config_dir())

    @property
    def term(self) -> Terminal:
        # Built on first use, so it writes to the streams in place when the command runs.
        if self._terminal is None:
            self._terminal = Terminal.for_streams(sys.stdout, sys.stderr, self.color)
        return self._terminal

    @property
    def is_interactive(self) -> bool:
        if self.interactive is not None:
            return self.interactive
        try:
            return sys.stdin.isatty()
        except (AttributeError, ValueError):
            return False

    def client(self) -> httpx2.Client:
        if self.http is None:
            self.http = httpx2.Client(base_url=api_url(), timeout=TIMEOUT)
        return self.http

    def anonymous_api(self) -> Api:
        return Api(self.client())

    def api(self) -> Api:
        """An API client for the logged-in user. A 401 clears the saved session."""
        credentials = self.store.load()
        if credentials is None:
            raise NotLoggedIn()
        return Api(self.client(), credentials.token, on_session_ended=self.store.clear)
"""Failures the CLI reports itself, before or instead of asking the server, and the exit codes
every command uses."""

from collections.abc import Sequence

OK = 0
REFUSED = 1
USAGE = 2
AUTHENTICATION = 3
UNAVAILABLE = 4
INTERRUPTED = 130


class CliError(Exception):
    """Something to tell the user, with an optional next step, and the exit code to use."""

    exit_code = REFUSED

    def __init__(self, message: str, *, hint: str | None = None, bullets: Sequence[str] = ()):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.message = message
        self.hint = hint
        self.bullets = tuple(bullets)


class Refused(CliError):
    """A reference that doesn't match anything, e.g. an email nobody has."""


class Declined(CliError):
    """The user answered no to a confirmation."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__("Nothing was changed.")


class UsageProblem(CliError):
    """Input the CLI can't use, found before anything was sent."""

    exit_code = USAGE


class NotLoggedIn(CliError):
    exit_code = AUTHENTICATION

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__("You're not logged in.", hint="Log in: mc login")
"""The `mc` entry point: every command group, the global options, and a quiet exit when the
reader of a pipe goes away."""

import os
import sys
from importlib.metadata import PackageNotFoundError, version
from typing import Annotated

import typer

from mission_control.cli.commands import account, admin, assignments, crew, me, missions
from mission_control.cli.commands.common import group
from mission_control.cli.env import Env

app = group(name="mc")
account.register(app)
app.add_typer(me.app, name="me")
app.add_typer(assignments.app, name="assignments")
app.add_typer(missions.app, name="missions")
app.add_typer(crew.app, name="crew")
app.add_typer(admin.users_app, name="users")
app.add_typer(admin.skills_app, name="skills")
app.add_typer(admin.org_app, name="org")


def _show_version(value: bool) -> None:
    if value:
        try:
            number = version("mission-control")
        except PackageNotFoundError:
            number = "unknown"
        typer.echo(f"mc {number}")
        raise typer.Exit()


@app.callback()
def root(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    show_version: Annotated[
        bool,
        typer.Option(
            "--version", help="Show the version and exit", callback=_show_version, is_eager=True
        ),
    ] = False,
) -> None:
    """Mission Control: plan missions, crew them and get them approved.

    Start with mc login, then mc inbox. Every command has --help.

    Exit codes: 0 OK, 1 refused, 2 usage, 3 not logged in, 4 server unreachable or busy,
    130 interrupted.
    """
    if ctx.obj is None:
        ctx.obj = Env()


def main() -> None:
    try:
        app(prog_name="mc")
    finally:
        _flush_quietly()


def _flush_quietly() -> None:
    """`mc ... | head` closes the pipe early; don't end with a broken-pipe traceback."""
    try:
        sys.stdout.flush()
    except BrokenPipeError:
        devnull = os.open(os.devnull, os.O_WRONLY)
        os.dup2(devnull, sys.stdout.fileno())
"""Asking the user things. Prompts go to the terminal (stderr, or the tty for passwords), never to
stdout. Passwords are read with the input hidden, or from stdin, one line per prompt, when
there's no terminal."""

import getpass
import sys
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Protocol

from mission_control.cli.errors import Declined, UsageProblem

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from mission_control.cli.env import Env


class Prompter(Protocol):
    def ask(self, label: str) -> str: ...

    def ask_hidden(self, label: str) -> str: ...


class TerminalPrompter:
    """Reads answers from the keyboard. Ctrl+C raises KeyboardInterrupt; Ctrl+D is treated the
    same, as a way out."""

    def ask(self, label: str) -> str:
        sys.stderr.write(f"{label}: ")
        sys.stderr.flush()
        line = sys.stdin.readline()
        if not line:
            raise KeyboardInterrupt
        return line.rstrip("\r\n")

    def ask_hidden(self, label: str) -> str:
        try:
            return getpass.getpass(f"{label}: ")
        except EOFError:
            raise KeyboardInterrupt from None


def ask(env: "Env", label: str, *, missing: str) -> str:
    """A visible answer. Without a terminal, fail with `missing` instead of waiting."""
    if not env.is_interactive:
        raise UsageProblem(missing)
    while not (answer := env.prompter.ask(label).strip()):
        pass
    return answer


def ask_secret(env: "Env", label: str) -> str:
    if env.is_interactive:
        return env.prompter.ask_hidden(label)
    line = sys.stdin.readline()
    if not line:
        raise UsageProblem(
            "No password given, and there's no terminal to ask on.",
            hint="Run this in a terminal, or pipe it on stdin.",
        )
    return line.rstrip("\r\n")


def ask_new_secret(env: "Env", label: str, repeat: str, *, mismatch: str) -> str:
    """A password being set: asked twice, and both answers must match."""
    first = ask_secret(env, label)
    if first != ask_secret(env, repeat):
        raise UsageProblem(mismatch)
    return first


def confirm(env: "Env", warning: str, *, yes: bool, question: str = "Continue?") -> None:
    """Show `warning` and ask before going ahead. `--yes` skips the question. Without a terminal
    the change needs `--yes`, so a script never waits on a prompt."""
    if yes:
        return
    env.term.warn(warning)
    if not env.is_interactive:
        raise UsageProblem(
            "There's no terminal to confirm on.", hint="Re-run with --yes to go ahead."
        )
    if env.prompter.ask(f"{question} [y/N]").strip().lower() not in ("y", "yes"):
        raise Declined()

"""Turning a refusal into what the user reads: the message, one bullet per problem that adds
something, and a next step for the codes we know."""

from collections.abc import Generator, Mapping
from contextlib import contextmanager
from dataclasses import dataclass

from mission_control.cli.client import ApiError, Unreachable
from mission_control.cli.env import Env
from mission_control.cli.errors import INTERRUPTED, UNAVAILABLE, CliError
from mission_control.cli.render import sentence

# A mission's state stopped the action. The message says "a mission", so the CLI adds which one.
STATE_CODES = frozenset({"mission_frozen", "mission_final", "invalid_transition"})

GENERIC_HINTS = {
    "unauthenticated": "Log in again: mc login",
    "busy": "Try again in a moment.",
    "unexpected_response": "Try again. If it keeps happening, check the server's log.",
}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Shown:
    message: str
    bullets: tuple[str, ...]
    hints: tuple[str, ...]


@contextmanager
def refusals(
    subject: str | None = None, *, name_state: bool = False, **hints: str
) -> Generator[None]:
    """Give refusals raised inside the block the context only the command knows: the `subject`
    (e.g. "Mission 5") for a not-found message, whether a state refusal should name it, and a
    hint per code. A hint may use `{name}` placeholders filled from the problem's refs."""
    try:
        yield
    except ApiError as error:
        if error.subject is None:
            error.subject = subject
            error.name_state = name_state
        error.hints = hints | error.hints
        raise


def present(error: ApiError) -> Shown:
    message = error.message
    subject = error.subject
    if subject:
        kind = subject.split()[0]
        if error.code == "not_found" and message.lower() == f"{kind} not found".lower():
            message = f"{subject} not found"
        elif error.code in STATE_CODES and error.name_state:
            message = f"{subject}: {message}"

    bullets = tuple(p.message for p in error.problems if p.message and p.message != error.message)

    hints: list[str] = []
    for code, refs in ((error.code, {}), *((p.code, p.refs) for p in error.problems)):
        hint = _fill(error.hints.get(code) or GENERIC_HINTS.get(code), refs)
        if hint and hint not in hints:
            hints.append(hint)
    return Shown(sentence(message), bullets, tuple(hints))


def _fill(template: str | None, refs: Mapping[str, object]) -> str | None:
    """A hint may name a record from the problem's refs, e.g. `{assignment_id}`. Without that
    ref the hint is skipped rather than shown half-filled."""
    if template is None:
        return None
    try:
        return template.format_map(dict(refs))
    except (KeyError, IndexError, ValueError):
        return None


def report(env: Env, failure: BaseException) -> int:
    """Show a failure on stderr (or a refusal's error body on stdout, with `--json`) and return
    the exit code to use."""
    term = env.term
    if isinstance(failure, KeyboardInterrupt):
        term.say()
        term.say("Cancelled.")
        return INTERRUPTED
    if isinstance(failure, Unreachable):
        term.error(
            f"Can't reach the Mission Control server at {failure.url}.",
            hint="Is it running? Start it with: uv run mc-server",
        )
        return UNAVAILABLE
    if isinstance(failure, ApiError):
        if env.json_output and failure.has_error_body:
            term.json(failure.body)
        else:
            shown = present(failure)
            term.error(shown.message, shown.bullets, shown.hints)
        return failure.exit_code
    if isinstance(failure, CliError):
        term.error(sentence(failure.message), failure.bullets, failure.hint)
        return failure.exit_code
    raise failure
"""How API records read on screen, shared by the command groups."""

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Any

from mission_control.cli.render import MISSION_STATUS, ROLE, Terminal


def mission_status(mission: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return MISSION_STATUS.get(mission["status"], mission["status"])


def mission_heading(term: Terminal, mission: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    """`Lunar Gateway Resupply · 1–10 Mar 2027 · Draft`."""
    dates = term.dates(mission["start_date"], mission["end_date"])
    return term.heading(mission["name"], dates, mission_status(mission))


def mission_row(term: Terminal, mission: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    return [
        str(mission["id"]),
        mission["name"],
        term.dates(mission["start_date"], mission["end_date"]),
        mission_status(mission),
    ]


def org_name(me: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str | None:
    org = me.get("organisation")
    return org.get("name") if isinstance(org, Mapping) else None


def identity(me: Mapping[str, Any], name: str | None = None) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    pairs = [
        ("Name", name or me["name"]),
        ("Email", me["email"]),
        ("Role", ROLE.get(me["role"], me["role"])),
    ]
    if org := org_name(me):
        pairs.append(("Organisation", org))
    return pairs


def requirements_text(requirements: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
    """`Piloting>=4, Leadership>=3`, the same form `--slot` takes."""
    return ", ".join(f"{r['skill_name']}>={r['min_level']}" for r in requirements)


def skills_text(skills: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
    return ", ".join(f"{s['skill_name']} {s['level']}" for s in skills)


def assignment_summary_rows(term: Terminal, rows: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> list[list[str]]:
    return [
        [
            str(a["assignment_id"]),
            a["mission_name"],
            a["slot_name"],
            term.dates(a["start_date"], a["end_date"]),
            a["status"],
        ]
        for a in rows
    ]


def show_profile(
    term: Terminal,
    pairs: list[tuple[str, str]],
    profile: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    own: bool,
) -> None:
    """A crew profile: details, skills, and unavailability. Reasons appear only when the
    server sent them; IDs only on your own, since you need them to remove an entry."""
    term.fields(pairs + [("Bio", profile.get("bio") or "none")])
    term.line()
    skills = profile.get("skills") or []
    if skills:
        term.table(["Skill", "Level"], [[s["skill_name"], str(s["level"])] for s in skills])
    else:
        term.line("No skills recorded yet.")
    term.line()
    unavailable = profile.get("unavailability") or []
    if not unavailable:
        term.line("No unavailability recorded.")
        return
    with_reasons = any(u.get("reason") for u in unavailable)
    headers = (["ID"] if own else []) + ["Unavailable"] + (["Reason"] if with_reasons else [])
    rows = []
    for u in unavailable:
        row = ([str(u["id"])] if own else []) + [term.dates(u["start_date"], u["end_date"])]
        rows.append(row + ([u.get("reason") or ""] if with_reasons else []))
    term.table(headers, rows)
"""Turning what the user types into what the API expects. Missions and assignments are IDs;
people are found by email, skills by name and slots by name or position. Also parses the
`--slot` spec, e.g. `Commander:1:Piloting>=4,Leadership>=3`.

Only syntax is checked here. Whether a headcount or level is allowed is the server's call."""

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from mission_control.cli.client import Api
from mission_control.cli.errors import Refused, UsageProblem

SLOT_FORMAT = "NAME:HEADCOUNT:SKILL>=LEVEL[,SKILL>=LEVEL...]"
SLOT_EXAMPLE = "Commander:1:Piloting>=4,Leadership>=3"
REQUIREMENTS_FORMAT = "SKILL>=LEVEL[,SKILL>=LEVEL...]"


class SpecError(UsageProblem):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SlotSpec:
    name: str
    headcount: int
    requirements: tuple[tuple[str, int], ...]  # (skill name, minimum level), as typed


def parse_slot_spec(text: str) -> SlotSpec:
    parts = text.rsplit(":", 2)
    if len(parts) != 3 or not parts[0].strip():
        raise _slot_error(text)
    name, headcount, requirements = (p.strip() for p in parts)
    try:
        count = int(headcount)
    except ValueError:
        raise _slot_error(text) from None
    try:
        parsed = parse_requirements(requirements) if requirements else ()
    except SpecError:
        raise _slot_error(text) from None
    return SlotSpec(name, count, parsed)


def parse_requirements(text: str) -> tuple[tuple[str, int], ...]:
    requirements = []
    for entry in text.split(","):
        skill, sep, level = entry.partition(">=")
        skill, level = skill.strip(), level.strip()
        if not sep or not skill or not level.lstrip("-").isdigit():
            raise SpecError(
                f"'{entry.strip()}' isn't a requirement. Use {REQUIREMENTS_FORMAT}, "
                "e.g. Piloting>=4,Leadership>=3."
            )
        requirements.append((skill, int(level)))
    return tuple(requirements)


def _slot_error(text: str) -> SpecError:
    return SpecError(f"'{text}' isn't a slot. Use {SLOT_FORMAT}, e.g. {SLOT_EXAMPLE}.")


# References


def skill_ids(api: Api, names: Sequence[str]) -> dict[str, int]:
    """Skill names (any case) to IDs, from the organisation's catalogue."""
    catalogue = {s["name"].casefold(): s for s in api.get("/skills")}
    found: dict[str, int] = {}
    for name in names:
        skill = catalogue.get(name.casefold())
        if skill is None:
            raise Refused(
                f"There's no skill called '{name}'", hint="See the skills: mc skills list"
            )
        found[name] = skill["id"]
    return found


def skill_named(api: Api, name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    for skill in api.get("/skills"):
        if skill["name"].casefold() == name.casefold():
            return skill
    raise Refused(f"There's no skill called '{name}'", hint="See the skills: mc skills list")


def requirement_body(
    requirements: Sequence[tuple[str, int]], ids: Mapping[str, int]
) -> list[dict[str, int]]:
    return [{"skill_id": ids[name], "min_level": level} for name, level in requirements]


def crew_by_email(api: Api, email: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    for person in api.get("/crew"):
        if person["email"].casefold() == email.strip().casefold():
            return person
    raise Refused(
        f"There's no crew member with the email {email}", hint="See the crew: mc crew list"
    )


def user_by_email(api: Api, email: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    for user in api.get("/users"):
        if user["email"].casefold() == email.strip().casefold():
            return user
    raise Refused(f"There's no user with the email {email}", hint="See the users: mc users list")


def slot_in(mission: Mapping[str, Any], ref: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """A live slot by position (`2`) or by name, ignoring case (`Flight Engineer`)."""
    slots = mission["slots"]
    ref = ref.strip()
    if ref.isdigit():
        match = [s for s in slots if s["position"] == int(ref)]
        missing = f"{mission['name']} has no slot {ref}"
    else:
        match = [s for s in slots if s["name"].casefold() == ref.casefold()]
        missing = f"{mission['name']} has no slot called '{ref}'"
    if not match:
        listed = ", ".join(f"{s['position']} {s['name']}" for s in slots)
        raise Refused(missing, hint=f"Its slots: {listed}")
    return match[0]
"""Plumbing every command shares: the group and command classes (help layout, error handling),
the current `Env`, and common options."""

from datetime import date
from typing import Annotated, Any

import typer
from typer.core import TyperCommand, TyperGroup

from mission_control.cli.client import ApiError, Unreachable
from mission_control.cli.env import Env
from mission_control.cli.errors import CliError
from mission_control.cli.refusals import report


class Command(TyperCommand):
    """Usage lines that spell out the options (`mc missions match MISSION_ID [--apply]`), and
    the epilog shown as an example block."""

    def collect_usage_pieces(self, ctx: typer.Context) -> list[str]:
        pieces: list[str] = []
        options: list[str] = []
        for param in self.get_params(ctx):
            if param.param_type_name == "argument":
                pieces.append(param.make_metavar(ctx))
            elif param.param_type_name == "option" and param.name != "help" and not param.hidden:
                flag = max(param.opts, key=len)
                text = flag if param.is_flag else f"{flag} {param.make_metavar(ctx)}"
                options.append(text if param.required else f"[{text}]")
        return pieces + options

    def format_epilog(self, ctx: typer.Context, formatter: Any) -> None:
        if not self.epilog:
            return
        examples = [line.strip() for line in self.epilog.strip().splitlines() if line.strip()]
        formatter.write_paragraph()
        formatter.write_text("Example:" if len(examples) == 1 else "Examples:")
        with formatter.indentation():
            for line in examples:
                formatter.write(f"{' ' * formatter.current_indent}{line}\n")


class Group(TyperGroup):
    """A bare group prints its help and exits 0. Failures raised by commands are reported here
    with their exit code, so tests and real runs behave the same."""

    def parse_args(self, ctx: typer.Context, args: list[str]) -> list[str]:
        if not args and not ctx.resilient_parsing:
            typer.echo(ctx.get_help())
            ctx.exit(0)
        return super().parse_args(ctx, args)

    def invoke(self, ctx: typer.Context) -> Any:
        try:
            return super().invoke(ctx)
        except (CliError, ApiError, Unreachable, KeyboardInterrupt) as failure:
            env = ctx.find_object(Env) or Env()
            raise typer.Exit(report(env, failure)) from None


def group(help: str | None = None, name: str | None = None) -> typer.Typer:
    """A command group. Without `help`, its callback's docstring is the help."""
    named: dict[str, str] = {"help": help} if help else {}
    if name:
        named["name"] = name
    return typer.Typer(
        cls=Group,
        rich_markup_mode=None,
        add_completion=False,
        pretty_exceptions_enable=False,
        **named,
    )


def start(ctx: typer.Context, json_output: bool = False) -> Env:
    """The command's Env, remembering whether output is JSON so refusals print as JSON too."""
    env = ctx.ensure_object(Env)
    env.json_output = json_output
    return env


def iso_date(value: str | None) -> str | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    try:
        return date.fromisoformat(value).isoformat()
    except ValueError:
        raise typer.BadParameter(
            f"'{value}' isn't a date. Use YYYY-MM-DD, e.g. 2027-03-01."
        ) from None


Json = Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--json", help="Print the result as JSON")]
Yes = Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--yes", "-y", help="Don't ask for confirmation")]
MissionId = Annotated[int, typer.Argument(metavar="MISSION_ID", help="The mission's ID")]
Email = Annotated[str, typer.Argument(metavar="EMAIL", help="The person's email address")]


def command(app: typer.Typer, name: str, example: str | None = None, **settings: Any) -> Any:
    """Register a command with the shared help layout. `example` lines appear under Example."""
    return app.command(name, cls=Command, epilog=example, **settings)

"""Who may do what. `authorize` is the single entry point; it raises Forbidden with a code.

Each action combines a role check with, where needed, a relationship check: owner of the
mission, the person themselves, the mission's creator, or someone on its roster.
"""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from types import MappingProxyType

from mission_control.domain.approvals import conflict_problems
from mission_control.domain.enums import Action, Role
from mission_control.domain.errors import Forbidden


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Actor:
    user_id: int
    org_id: int
    role: Role
    crew_profile_id: int | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Context:
    """Facts about the target that the relationship rules need. Unused fields stay None.

    None means "not supplied", which differs from an empty roster ("nobody rostered"). An action
    whose rule needs a field refuses to run without it rather than treating it as a mismatch.
    """

    owner_id: int | None = None  # the mission's owning Lead
    created_by_id: int | None = None  # who created the mission
    target_user_id: int | None = None  # whose profile, skills or assignment this is
    rostered_user_ids: frozenset[int] | None = None


D, L, C = Role.DIRECTOR, Role.LEAD, Role.CREW
ALL = frozenset(Role)

# Which roles may attempt each action at all. Relationship rules below narrow this further.
ROLES: Mapping[Action, frozenset[Role]] = MappingProxyType(
    {
        Action.MANAGE_ORG: frozenset({D}),
        Action.MANAGE_SKILLS: frozenset({D}),
        Action.LIST_SKILLS: frozenset({D, L}),
        Action.CHANGE_OWN_PASSWORD: ALL,
        Action.VIEW_CREW: ALL,
        Action.VIEW_ASSIGNMENT_HISTORY: ALL,
        Action.VIEW_UNAVAILABILITY_REASON: ALL,
        Action.EDIT_OWN_PROFILE: ALL,
        Action.SET_SKILL_LEVEL: frozenset({D, L}),
        Action.VIEW_MISSION: ALL,
        Action.CREATE_MISSION: frozenset({D, L}),
        Action.EDIT_MISSION: frozenset({L}),
        Action.PROPOSE_CREW: frozenset({L}),
        Action.REMOVE_CREW: frozenset({L}),
        Action.APPLY_MATCH: frozenset({L}),
        Action.SUBMIT: frozenset({L}),
        Action.PREVIEW_MATCH: frozenset({D, L}),
        Action.COMPLETE: frozenset({D, L}),
        Action.CANCEL: frozenset({D, L}),
        Action.TRANSFER: frozenset({D}),
        Action.APPROVE: frozenset({D}),
        Action.REJECT: frozenset({D}),
        Action.ACCEPT: ALL,
        Action.DECLINE: ALL,
        Action.WITHDRAW: ALL,
    }
)

_OWNER_ONLY = frozenset(
    {
        Action.EDIT_MISSION,
        Action.PROPOSE_CREW,
        Action.REMOVE_CREW,
        Action.APPLY_MATCH,
        Action.SUBMIT,
    }
)
_OWNER_OR_DIRECTOR = frozenset({Action.PREVIEW_MATCH, Action.COMPLETE, Action.CANCEL})
# Crew may only see their own record; Leads and Directors may see anyone's.
_CREW_SELF_ONLY = frozenset({Action.VIEW_CREW, Action.VIEW_ASSIGNMENT_HISTORY})
# Only the person themselves, whatever their role.
_SELF_ONLY = frozenset({Action.EDIT_OWN_PROFILE, Action.ACCEPT, Action.DECLINE, Action.WITHDRAW})

_OWNER = ("owner_id",)
_TARGET = ("target_user_id",)
# The Context fields each relationship rule reads. Leaving one out is a caller bug, not a refusal.
_REQUIRED_CONTEXT: Mapping[Action, tuple[str, ...]] = MappingProxyType(
    {
        Action.EDIT_MISSION: _OWNER,
        Action.PROPOSE_CREW: _OWNER,
        Action.REMOVE_CREW: _OWNER,
        Action.APPLY_MATCH: _OWNER,
        Action.SUBMIT: _OWNER,
        Action.PREVIEW_MATCH: _OWNER,
        Action.COMPLETE: _OWNER,
        Action.CANCEL: _OWNER,
        Action.CREATE_MISSION: _OWNER,
        Action.SET_SKILL_LEVEL: _TARGET,
        Action.EDIT_OWN_PROFILE: _TARGET,
        Action.ACCEPT: _TARGET,
        Action.DECLINE: _TARGET,
        Action.WITHDRAW: _TARGET,
        Action.VIEW_CREW: _TARGET,
        Action.VIEW_ASSIGNMENT_HISTORY: _TARGET,
        Action.VIEW_UNAVAILABILITY_REASON: _TARGET,
        Action.APPROVE: ("created_by_id", "rostered_user_ids"),
        Action.REJECT: ("created_by_id", "rostered_user_ids"),
        Action.VIEW_MISSION: ("rostered_user_ids",),
    }
)


# Messages. Phrases are written out so every refusal reads as a natural sentence.

_ROLE_PLURAL: Mapping[Role, str] = MappingProxyType({D: "Directors", L: "Leads", C: "Crew"})

# Completes "<Role plural> can't ...".
ROLE_PHRASE: Mapping[Action, str] = MappingProxyType(
    {
        Action.MANAGE_ORG: "manage the organisation",
        Action.MANAGE_SKILLS: "manage the skill catalogue",
        Action.LIST_SKILLS: "view the skill catalogue",
        Action.CHANGE_OWN_PASSWORD: "change their password",
        Action.VIEW_CREW: "view crew profiles",
        Action.VIEW_ASSIGNMENT_HISTORY: "view assignment history",
        Action.VIEW_UNAVAILABILITY_REASON: "see unavailability reasons",
        Action.EDIT_OWN_PROFILE: "edit profiles",
        Action.SET_SKILL_LEVEL: "change skill levels",
        Action.VIEW_MISSION: "view missions",
        Action.CREATE_MISSION: "create missions",
        Action.EDIT_MISSION: "edit missions",
        Action.PROPOSE_CREW: "propose crew",
        Action.REMOVE_CREW: "remove crew",
        Action.APPLY_MATCH: "apply suggested crew",
        Action.SUBMIT: "submit missions",
        Action.PREVIEW_MATCH: "see suggested crew",
        Action.COMPLETE: "complete missions",
        Action.CANCEL: "cancel missions",
        Action.TRANSFER: "transfer mission ownership",
        Action.APPROVE: "approve missions",
        Action.REJECT: "reject missions",
        Action.ACCEPT: "accept assignments",
        Action.DECLINE: "decline assignments",
        Action.WITHDRAW: "withdraw from assignments",
    }
)

# Completes "Only the mission's owner can ..." / "Only the owner or a Director can ...".
_MISSION_PHRASE: Mapping[Action, str] = MappingProxyType(
    {
        Action.EDIT_MISSION: "edit this mission",
        Action.PROPOSE_CREW: "propose crew for this mission",
        Action.REMOVE_CREW: "remove crew from this mission",
        Action.APPLY_MATCH: "apply suggested crew to this mission",
        Action.SUBMIT: "submit this mission",
        Action.PREVIEW_MATCH: "see suggested crew for this mission",
        Action.COMPLETE: "complete this mission",
        Action.CANCEL: "cancel this mission",
    }
)

_RESPOND_ONLY_TO_OWN = "You can only respond to your own assignments"
_SELF_ONLY_MESSAGE: Mapping[Action, str] = MappingProxyType(
    {
        Action.VIEW_CREW: "Crew can only view their own profile",
        Action.VIEW_ASSIGNMENT_HISTORY: "Crew can only view their own assignment history",
        Action.EDIT_OWN_PROFILE: "You can only edit your own profile and availability",
        Action.ACCEPT: _RESPOND_ONLY_TO_OWN,
        Action.DECLINE: _RESPOND_ONLY_TO_OWN,
        Action.WITHDRAW: _RESPOND_ONLY_TO_OWN,
    }
)


NO_CONTEXT = Context()


def authorize(actor: Actor, action: Action, ctx: Context = NO_CONTEXT) -> None:
    if actor.role not in ROLES[action]:
        raise Forbidden("role_forbidden", f"{_ROLE_PLURAL[actor.role]} can't {ROLE_PHRASE[action]}")

    missing = [name for name in _REQUIRED_CONTEXT.get(action, ()) if getattr(ctx, name) is None]
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(f"{action.value} needs context field(s): {', '.join(missing)}")

    rostered = ctx.rostered_user_ids or frozenset()
    is_self = ctx.target_user_id == actor.user_id
    is_owner = ctx.owner_id == actor.user_id

    if action in _OWNER_ONLY and not is_owner:
        raise Forbidden("not_owner", f"Only the mission's owner can {_MISSION_PHRASE[action]}")

    if action in _OWNER_OR_DIRECTOR and actor.role != D and not is_owner:
        raise Forbidden("not_owner", f"Only the owner or a Director can {_MISSION_PHRASE[action]}")

    if action == Action.CREATE_MISSION and actor.role == L and not is_owner:
        raise Forbidden("not_owner", "Leads can only create missions they own")

"""Everything the CLI prints. Data goes to stdout, messages and next steps to stderr. Colour only
marks a symbol, never carries meaning alone, and appears only on a terminal. With `NO_COLOR`,
`TERM=dumb` or a non-UTF-8 terminal the symbols become plain words."""

import json
import os
from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import UTC, date, datetime
from typing import Any, TextIO

from rich.cells import cell_len

_GAP = "   "
_INDENT = "  "

_GREEN, _YELLOW, _RED, _RESET = "\x1b[32m", "\x1b[33m", "\x1b[31m", "\x1b[0m"

MISSION_STATUS = {
    "draft": "Draft",
    "pending_approval": "Pending approval",
    "active": "Active",
    "completed": "Completed",
    "cancelled": "Cancelled",
}
ROLE = {"director": "Director", "lead": "Mission Lead", "crew": "Crew Member"}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Style:
    color: bool
    unicode: bool


def detect_style(stream: TextIO, environ: Mapping[str, str]) -> Style:
    """Colour needs a terminal, no `NO_COLOR` and a `TERM` that isn't dumb. Symbols need UTF-8
    and the same opt-outs."""
    term = environ.get("TERM", "")
    opted_out = "NO_COLOR" in environ or term == "dumb"
    is_tty = _isatty(stream)
    encoding = (getattr(stream, "encoding", None) or "").lower().replace("-", "")
    return Style(
        color=is_tty and not opted_out and bool(term),
        unicode=encoding == "utf8" and not opted_out,
    )


def _isatty(stream: TextIO) -> bool:
    try:
        return stream.isatty()
    except (AttributeError, ValueError):
        return False


# Dates


def parse_date(value: str) -> date:
    return date.fromisoformat(value)


def format_range(start: date, end: date, *, plain: bool = False) -> str:
    """`1–10 Mar 2027`, `25 Feb – 2 Mar 2027`, `30 Dec 2026 – 2 Jan 2027`, or one day."""
    dash = "-" if plain else "–"
    if start == end:
        return _day(end, year=True)
    if (start.year, start.month) == (end.year, end.month):
        return f"{start.day}{dash}{_day(end, year=True)}"
    left = _day(start, year=start.year != end.year)
    return f"{left} {dash} {_day(end, year=True)}"


def _day(d: date, *, year: bool) -> str:
    text = f"{d.day} {d.strftime('%b')}"
    return f"{text} {d.year}" if year else text


def format_timestamp(value: str) -> str:
    """A UTC timestamp from the server as `5 Jan 2027 09:03`."""
    moment = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00")).astimezone(UTC)
"""Your assignments: list them, and accept, decline or withdraw."""

from contextlib import AbstractContextManager
from typing import Annotated, Any

import typer

from mission_control.cli.client import Api
from mission_control.cli.commands.common import Json, Yes, command, group, start
from mission_control.cli.prompts import confirm
from mission_control.cli.refusals import refusals
from mission_control.cli.views import assignment_summary_rows

app = group("Your assignments: accept, decline or withdraw.")

AssignmentId = Annotated[
    int, typer.Argument(metavar="ASSIGNMENT_ID", help="From mc assignments list or mc inbox")
]


def _refusals(assignment_id: int) -> AbstractContextManager[None]:
    return refusals(
        f"Assignment {assignment_id}", not_found="See your assignments: mc assignments list"
    )


def _mine(api: Api, assignment_id: int) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Your summary of the assignment (mission, slot, mission status), if it's yours."""
    for row in api.get("/assignments"):
        if row["assignment_id"] == assignment_id:
            return row
    return None


def _what(summary: dict[str, Any] | None, assignment_id: int) -> str:
    if summary is None:
        return f"assignment {assignment_id}"
    return f"{summary['slot_name']} on {summary['mission_name']}"


@command(app, "list", example="mc assignments list --pending")
def list_assignments(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    pending: Annotated[
        bool, typer.Option("--pending", help="Only proposals waiting for your answer")
    ] = False,
    json_output: Json = False,
) -> None:
    """List your assignments."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    rows = env.api().get("/assignments", {"pending": "true"} if pending else None)
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(rows)
        return
    if not rows:
        env.term.line(
            "No proposals are waiting for you." if pending else "You have no assignments."
        )
        return
    env.term.table(
        ["ID", "Mission", "Slot", "Dates", "Status"], assignment_summary_rows(env.term, rows)
    )


@command(app, "accept", example="mc assignments accept 21")
def accept(ctx: typer.Context, assignment_id: AssignmentId, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Accept a proposal. Only while the mission is a draft."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    with _refusals(assignment_id):
        summary = _mine(api, assignment_id)
        result = api.post(f"/assignments/{assignment_id}/accept")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(result)
        return
    env.term.ok(f"Accepted {_what(summary, assignment_id)}.")


@command(app, "decline", example='mc assignments decline 21 --reason "On leave"')
def decline(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    assignment_id: AssignmentId,
    reason: Annotated[
        str | None, typer.Option("--reason", metavar="TEXT", help="Why (optional)")
    ] = None,
    json_output: Json = False,
) -> None:
    """Decline a proposal."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    with _refusals(assignment_id):
        summary = _mine(api, assignment_id)
        body = {"reason": reason} if reason is not None else None
        result = api.post(f"/assignments/{assignment_id}/decline", body)
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(result)
        return
    env.term.ok(f"Declined {_what(summary, assignment_id)}.")


@command(app, "withdraw", example='mc assignments withdraw 31 --reason "Family matter"')
def withdraw(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    assignment_id: AssignmentId,
    reason: Annotated[str, typer.Option("--reason", metavar="TEXT", help="Why you're withdrawing")],
    yes: Yes = False,
    json_output: Json = False,
) -> None:
    """Withdraw from an accepted assignment. A mission pending approval goes back to Draft."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    with _refusals(assignment_id):
        summary = _mine(api, assignment_id)
        pending = summary is not None and summary["mission_status"] == "pending_approval"
        if pending:
            confirm(
                env,
                f"{summary['mission_name']} is pending approval. Withdrawing sends it back to "
                "Draft and clears its approvals.",
                yes=yes,
            )
        result = api.post(f"/assignments/{assignment_id}/withdraw", {"reason": reason})
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(result)
        return
    back = " It's back in Draft." if pending else ""
    env.term.ok(f"Withdrew from {_what(summary, assignment_id)}.{back}")
"""Your own crew profile: details, skills and unavailability."""

from typing import Annotated, Any

import typer

from mission_control.cli.client import Api
from mission_control.cli.commands.common import Json, command, group, iso_date, start
from mission_control.cli.errors import Refused, UsageProblem
from mission_control.cli.render import Terminal
from mission_control.cli.views import identity, show_profile

app = group("Your profile and availability.")
unavailable_app = group("Dates you can't fly.")
app.add_typer(unavailable_app, name="unavailable")

AwayFrom = Annotated[
    str,
    typer.Option("--start", metavar="YYYY-MM-DD", callback=iso_date, help="First day you're away"),
]
AwayTo = Annotated[
    str,
    typer.Option("--end", metavar="YYYY-MM-DD", callback=iso_date, help="Last day you're away"),
]
AwayReason = Annotated[
    str, typer.Option("--reason", metavar="TEXT", help="Why (only Directors and you see it)")
]


def _profile_id(api: Api) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], int]:
    me = api.get("/me")
    profile_id = me.get("crew_profile_id")
    if not profile_id:
        raise Refused(
            "You don't have a crew profile",
            hint="A Director can give you one: mc users enable-crew EMAIL",
        )
    return me, profile_id


@command(app, "show", example="mc me show")
def show(ctx: typer.Context, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Show your details, skills and unavailability."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    me = api.get("/me")
    profile = api.get(f"/crew/{me['crew_profile_id']}") if me.get("crew_profile_id") else None
    if json_output:
        env.term.json({"user": me, "crew_profile": profile})
        return
    if profile is None:
        env.term.fields(identity(me) + [("Crew profile", "none")])
        return
    show_profile(env.term, identity(me, profile["name"]), profile, own=True)


@command(app, "update", example='mc me update --name "Ben Ito" --bio "EVA and systems"')
def update(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    name: Annotated[
        str | None, typer.Option("--name", metavar="NAME", help="Your display name")
    ] = None,
    bio: Annotated[str | None, typer.Option("--bio", metavar="TEXT", help="A short bio")] = None,
    json_output: Json = False,
) -> None:
    """Change your display name or bio."""
    if name is None and bio is None:
        raise UsageProblem("Nothing to change. Pass --name or --bio.")
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    _, profile_id = _profile_id(api)
    body = {"display_name": name, "bio": bio}
    profile = api.patch(f"/crew/{profile_id}", {k: v for k, v in body.items() if v is not None})
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(profile)
        return
    env.term.ok("Updated your profile.")


@command(
    unavailable_app,
    "add",
    example="mc me unavailable add --start 2027-06-01 --end 2027-06-03 --reason Leave",
)
def add_unavailability(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    start_date: AwayFrom,
    end_date: AwayTo,
    reason: AwayReason,
    json_output: Json = False,
) -> None:
    """Record dates you're unavailable. Both days count."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    _, profile_id = _profile_id(api)
    added = api.post(
        f"/crew/{profile_id}/unavailability",
        {"start_date": start_date, "end_date": end_date, "reason": reason},
    )
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(added)
        return
    dates = env.term.dates(added["start_date"], added["end_date"])
    env.term.ok(f"Added unavailability {added['id']}: {dates} ({added['reason']}).")


@command(unavailable_app, "list", example="mc me unavailable list")
def list_unavailability(ctx: typer.Context, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """List the dates you've said you're unavailable."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    _, profile_id = _profile_id(api)
    entries = api.get(f"/crew/{profile_id}/unavailability")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(entries)
        return
    _unavailability_table(env.term, entries)


@command(unavailable_app, "remove", example="mc me unavailable remove 4")
def remove_unavailability(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    unavailability_id: Annotated[
        int, typer.Argument(metavar="ID", help="From mc me unavailable list")
    ],
) -> None:
    """Remove one of your unavailability entries."""
    env = start(ctx)
    api = env.api()
    _, profile_id = _profile_id(api)
    api.delete(f"/crew/{profile_id}/unavailability/{unavailability_id}")
    env.term.ok(f"Removed unavailability {unavailability_id}.")


def _unavailability_table(term: Terminal, entries: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    if not entries:
        term.line("No unavailability recorded.")
        return
    term.table(
        ["ID", "Unavailable", "Reason"],
        [
            [str(u["id"]), term.dates(u["start_date"], u["end_date"]), u.get("reason") or ""]
            for u in entries
        ],
    )
```

"""The HTTP API: JSON under /api/v1, authenticated with `Authorization: Bearer <token>`."""

import os
from collections.abc import AsyncGenerator
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import FastAPI

from mission_control.api import errors
from mission_control.api.routers import admin, assignments, auth, crew, missions
from mission_control.infra import security
from mission_control.infra.db import connect, init_db
from mission_control.infra.settings import Settings

API_PREFIX = "/api/v1"


def create_app(settings: Settings | None = None) -> FastAPI:
    """Settings come from the environment unless given."""
    config = settings if settings is not None else Settings.from_env(os.environ)

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(_: FastAPI) -> AsyncGenerator[None]:
        conn = connect(config.database_path, busy_timeout_ms=config.busy_timeout_ms)
        try:
            init_db(conn)  # refuses an unsupported SQLite or schema; creates an empty schema
        finally:
            conn.close()
        # Login checks unknown emails against this hash, so it costs the same as a real check.
        # Making it now keeps the first such login as slow as every other.
        security.dummy_hash()
        yield

    app = FastAPI(title="Mission Control", lifespan=lifespan)
    app.state.settings = config
    errors.install(app)

    @app.get("/health")
    def health() -> dict[str, str]:
        return {"status": "ok"}

    for router in (auth.router, admin.router, crew.router, missions.router, assignments.router):
        app.include_router(router, prefix=API_PREFIX)
    return app


def main() -> None:
    import uvicorn

    uvicorn.run(create_app(), host="127.0.0.1", port=8000)

"""The CLI against the real API in-process (no stand-in): every read command renders the real
responses, and S4 runs end to end (PRD S4, AC-14, AC-15, AC-24, AC-27; design §11 CLI tests).
S1 runs through the installed binaries in test_readme_smoke.py.

from mission_control.infra import security
from mission_control.infra.db import connect, init_db

            logged_in = mc(
                "login", "--email", f"{who}@{org}.example", input=f"{DEMO_PASSWORD}\n", http=server
            )

            people[who] = mc

        return run

    return _as

"""Runtime settings, read from the environment once at startup.

- `MC_DATABASE_PATH`: the SQLite file (default `mission_control.db` in the working directory)
- `MC_DB_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS`: how long a request waits for the write lock (default 5000)
- `MC_TOKEN_TTL_HOURS`: how long a session lasts (default 12)
"""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Settings:
    database_path: Path = Path("mission_control.db")
    busy_timeout_ms: int = 5000
    token_ttl_hours: int = 12

    @classmethod
    def from_env(cls, env: Mapping[str, str]) -> "Settings":
        defaults = cls()
        return cls(
            database_path=Path(env.get("MC_DATABASE_PATH") or defaults.database_path),
            busy_timeout_ms=_whole(env, "MC_DB_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS", defaults.busy_timeout_ms, 0),
            token_ttl_hours=_whole(env, "MC_TOKEN_TTL_HOURS", defaults.token_ttl_hours, 1),
        )


def _whole(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str, default: int, minimum: int) -> int:
    raw = env.get(name)
    if raw is None or raw.strip() == "":
        return default
    try:
        value = int(raw)
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a whole number, not {raw!r}") from None
    if value < minimum:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be at least {minimum}, not {value}")
    return value

"""Shared test setup.

- Puts this directory on sys.path so tests can `import builders`.
- Swaps in cheap Argon2 settings for speed. Tests marked `real_argon2` keep the production
  settings, so those stay covered.
"""

import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from argon2 import PasswordHasher

_TESTS_DIR = str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent)
if _TESTS_DIR not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _TESTS_DIR)

_CHEAP_HASHER = PasswordHasher(time_cost=1, memory_cost=8, parallelism=1)


def pytest_configure(config):
    config.addinivalue_line("markers", "real_argon2: use production Argon2 settings")


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _cheap_argon2(request, monkeypatch):
    if request.node.get_closest_marker("real_argon2") is None:
        from mission_control.infra import security

        monkeypatch.setattr(security, "_hasher", _CHEAP_HASHER)

A fake response must be for a route that exists, use that route's success status, and\nhave a body the response model accepts with no fields the model doesn't have. Error responses\nmust have the design's error shape.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nfrom collections.abc import Mapping\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport httpx2\nfrom fastapi.routing import APIRoute\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, TypeAdapter, ValidationError\n\nfrom mission_control.api.app import API_PREFIX, create_app\nfrom mission_control.api.schemas.missions import MissionSummaryOut\nfrom mission_control.infra.settings import Settings\n\n# Fields the CLI already reads that the API is about to add. Each is allowed only while the model\n# lacks it; once the model has it, it's checked like any other field.\n_COMING: Mapping[type[BaseModel], frozenset[str]] = {MissionSummaryOut: frozenset({\"owner_name\"})}\n\n_ROUTES = [r for r in create_app(Settings()).routes if isinstance(r, APIRoute)]\n\n\nclass _Strict(BaseModel):\n    model_config = ConfigDict(extra=\"forbid\")\n\n\nclass _ProblemBody(_Strict):\n    code: str\n    message: str\n    refs: dict[str, Any]\n\n\nclass _ErrorDetail(_Strict):\n    code: str\n    message: str\n    problems: list[_ProblemBody]\n\n\nclass _ErrorBody(_Strict):\n    error: _ErrorDetail\n\n\ndef _route(method: str, path: str) -> APIRoute | None:\n    for route in _ROUTES:\n        if method in route.methods and route.path_regex.match(API_PREFIX + path):\n            return route\n    return None\n\n\ndef problems(method: str, path: str, response: httpx2.Response) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"What's wrong with a scripted response to `method path` (path without the /api/v1 prefix).\n    Empty when it's a response the real API could send.\"\"\"\n    where = f\"{method} {path}\"\n    route = _route(method, path)\n    if route is None:\n        return [f\"{where}: the API has no such endpoint\"]\n    status = response.status_code\n    body = json.loads(response.content) if response.content else None\n\n    if status >= 400:\n        if status == 422 and isinstance(body, dict) and \"detail\" in body:\n            return [f\"{where}: a 422 body must use the error shape, not FastAPI's 'detail'\"]\n        try:\n            _ErrorBody.model_validate(body)\n        except ValidationError as exc:\n            return [f\"{where}: error body doesn't match the error shape: {exc}\"]\n        if not body[\"error\"][\"problems\"]:\n            return [f\"{where}: an error lists at least one problem\"]\n        return []\n\n    expected = route.status_code or 200\n    if status != expected:\n        return [f\"{where}: answers {expected}, not {status}\"]\n    if expected == 204:\n        return [] if body is None else [f\"{where}: a 204 has no body\"]\n\n    adapter: TypeAdapter[Any] = TypeAdapter(route.response_model)\n    try:\n        parsed = adapter.validate_python(body)\n    except ValidationError as exc:\n        return [f\"{where}: body doesn't match {_name(route)}: {exc}\"]\n    return [f\"{where}: {p}\" for p in _unknown(body, parsed, \"body\")]\n\n\ndef _name(route: APIRoute) -> str:\n    model = route.response_model\n    return getattr(model, \"__name__\", None) or str(model)\n\n\ndef _unknown(sent: Any, parsed: Any, at: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Fields in `sent` that the parsed model doesn't have, anywhere in the tree.\"\"\"\n    found: list[str] = []\n    if isinstance(parsed, BaseModel) and isinstance(sent, dict):\n        fields = type(parsed).model_fields\n        allowed = set(fields) | _coming(type(parsed))\n        for key, value in sent.items():\n            if key not in allowed:\n                found.append(f\"{at}.{key} isn't in {type(parsed).__name__}\")\n            elif key in fields:\n                found += _unknown(value, getattr(parsed, key), f\"{at}.{key}\")\n    elif isinstance(parsed, list | tuple) and isinstance(sent, list):\n        for i, (s, p) in enumerate(zip(sent, parsed, strict=False)):\n            found += _unknown(s, p, f\"{at}[{i}]\")\n    elif isinstance(parsed, Mapping) and isinstance(sent, dict):\n        for key, value in sent.items():\n            found += _unknown(value, parsed.get(key), f\"{at}.{key}\")\n    return found\n\n\ndef _coming(model: type[BaseModel]) -> frozenset[str]:\n    names: set[str] = set()\n    for base, extra in _COMING.items():\n        if issubclass(model, base):\n            names |= extra - set(model.model_fields)\n    return frozenset(names)\n"
}
