Architecture, schema, API, CLI, the delivery plan (§9) and the implementation guidelines (§10).\n\nIf the PRD and the design conflict, or are silent on something, stop and ask. Never invent behaviour.\n\n## Workflow (every module)\n\n1. **One approved module at a time.** Work only on the module from design §9 that I have approved. Don't start anything else.\n2. **Tests first.** Write failing tests that reference the PRD IDs, then implement. Run the full suite and lint: `uv run pytest` and `uv run ruff check`.\n3. **Real verification.** Exercise the behaviour for real, for example with a REPL call, an HTTP request or a CLI command. Show the actual output. A passing test run alone isn't verification.\n4. **Read-only review.** Review the full diff against the PRD and design §10 without editing. Report findings, and fix them only once I agree.\n5. **Stop for approval.** Summarise what changed, the test and verification results, and the review findings, and propose a commit message. **Don't commit, and don't start the next module, until I approve.**\n\n## Waves (parallel modules in worktrees)\n\nWhen I approve a wave of independent modules (design §9), build them in parallel and keep reviews small:\n\n1. **One worktree per module.** Run `git worktree add .worktrees/<module> -b <module> main`, then `uv sync` inside it. Each agent edits only its own worktree and doesn't commit.\n2. **Same workflow per module.** Steps 2 to 4 above still apply, inside the worktree.\n3. **Review one branch at a time.** Show me only that module's diff against `main` (`git -C .worktrees/<module> diff main`, including new files), with its results.\n4. **After I approve that module:**\n   - commit on its branch;\n   - rebase onto `main` if `main` has moved, and re-run its tests;\n   - fast-forward `main`;\n   - run the full suite on `main`;\n   - remove the worktree and branch.\n\n   Only then present the next branch.\n\nNever push, add dependencies, change the schema, or add endpoints, error codes or CLI commands outside the design without asking first.\n",

cat > tests/cli/test_api_contract.py <<'EOF'
"""The check that keeps the CLI's scripted API honest (see api_contract.py)."""

import api_contract
import httpx2
from cli_fakes import CREW, LEE_ME, S2_PREVIEW, gateway

from mission_control.api.app import API_PREFIX, create_app
from mission_control.infra.settings import Settings


def _response(body=None, status=200):
    return httpx2.Response(status, json=body) if body is not None else httpx2.Response(status)


def test_contract_covers_every_api_route():
    spec = create_app(Settings()).openapi()["paths"]
    served = {
        (method.upper(), path.removeprefix(API_PREFIX))
        for path, operations in spec.items()
        if path.startswith(API_PREFIX)
        for method in operations
    }
    checked = {(m, r.path) for r in api_contract.ROUTES for m in r.methods}
    assert checked == served


def test_seed_shaped_records_match_the_real_models():
    assert api_contract.problems("GET", "/me", _response(LEE_ME)) == []
    assert api_contract.problems("GET", "/crew", _response(CREW)) == []
    assert api_contract.problems("GET", "/missions/1", _response(gateway())) == []
    assert api_contract.problems("GET", "/missions/1/match", _response(S2_PREVIEW)) == []


def test_an_unknown_field_is_drift():
    body = {**LEE_ME, "nickname": "Lee"}
    assert api_contract.problems("GET", "/me", _response(body)) == [
        "GET /me: body.nickname isn't in MeOut"
    ]


def test_a_nested_unknown_field_is_drift():
    body = [{**CREW[0], "skills": [{"skill_id": 1, "skill_name": "Piloting", "level": 5, "x": 1}]}]
    assert api_contract.problems("GET", "/crew", _response(body)) == [
        "GET /crew: body[0].skills[0].x isn't in SkillLevelOut"
    ]


def test_a_missing_field_is_drift():
    body = {k: v for k, v in LEE_ME.items() if k != "role"}
    [found] = api_contract.problems("GET", "/me", _response(body))
    assert found.startswith("GET /me: body doesn't match MeOut")


def test_an_endpoint_the_api_lacks_is_drift():
    assert api_contract.problems("GET", "/missions/1/crew", _response([])) == [
        "GET /missions/1/crew: the API has no such endpoint"
    ]


def test_the_success_status_must_match():
    assert api_contract.problems("POST", "/auth/logout", _response(status=200)) == [
        "POST /auth/logout: answers 204, not 200"
    ]


def test_errors_must_use_the_error_shape():
    good = {"error": {"code": "not_found", "message": "Mission not found", "problems": [
        {"code": "not_found", "message": "Mission not found", "refs": {}}
    ]}}
    assert api_contract.problems("GET", "/missions/9", _response(good, 404)) == []
    [found] = api_contract.problems("GET", "/missions/9", _response({"detail": "x"}, 404))
    assert "error shape" in found
EOF

            return route

def problems(method: str, path: str, response: httpx2.Response) -> list[str]:
    """What's wrong with a scripted response to `method path` (path without the /api/v1 prefix).
    Empty when it's a response the real API could send."""
    where = f"{method} {path}"
    route = _route(method, path)
    if route is None:
        return [f"{where}: the API has no such endpoint"]
    status = response.status_code
    body = json.loads(response.content) if response.content else None

    if status >= 400:
        if status == 422 and isinstance(body, dict) and "detail" in body:
            return [f"{where}: a 422 body must use the error shape, not FastAPI's 'detail'"]
        try:
            _ErrorBody.model_validate(body)
        except ValidationError as exc:
            return [f"{where}: error body doesn't match the error shape: {exc}"]
        if not body["error"]["problems"]:
            return [f"{where}: an error lists at least one problem"]
        return []

    expected = route.status_code or 200
    if status != expected:
        return [f"{where}: answers {expected}, not {status}"]
    if expected == 204:
        return [] if body is None else [f"{where}: a 204 has no body"]

    adapter: TypeAdapter[Any] = TypeAdapter(route.response_model)
    try:
        parsed = adapter.validate_python(body)
    except ValidationError as exc:
        return [f"{where}: body doesn't match {_name(route)}: {exc}"]
```

p='tests/cli/api_contract.py'

new='''    status = response.status_code
    if status == 500:
        return []  # an unhandled server fault: the real server sends plain text too
    try:
        body = json.loads(response.content) if response.content else None
    except ValueError:
        return [f"{where}: the body isn't JSON"]

    if status >= 400:
        try:
            _ErrorBody.model_validate(body)
        except ValidationError as exc:
            return [f"{where}: error body doesn't match the error shape: {exc}"]
        if not body["error"]["problems"]:
            return [f"{where}: an error lists at least one problem"]
        return []

'''
s=s.replace(old,new)
s=s.replace('''must have the design's error shape.''','''must have the design's error shape. A plain 500 is allowed, as the real server sends one for
an unhandled fault.''')

"""M16: the PRD scenarios S1 (golden path) and S4 (overlaps) driven through `mc`, one user after
another, against a small stateful stand-in for the API (PRD §9.3, AC-1, AC-3, AC-8, AC-14, AC-15).

The stand-in only keeps the state these flows touch, and answers with the design's response and
error shapes. Business rules are the server's; here they're scripted to the PRD's expected
outcomes, so the tests check what the CLI sends and how it reports each step.
"""

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import httpx2
from cli_fakes import CREW, SKILLS, USERS, FakeApi, error, gateway, ok, problem

PEOPLE = {
    "ruth@aurora.example": (1, "Ruth Adeyemi", "director", None),
    "marco@aurora.example": (2, "Marco Bianchi", "director", 2),
    "ingrid@aurora.example": (3, "Ingrid Holm", "director", None),
    "lee@aurora.example": (4, "Lee Park", "lead", None),
    "nora@aurora.example": (5, "Nora Quinn", "lead", None),
    "ana@aurora.example": (6, "Ana Okafor", "lead", 6),
    "chen@aurora.example": (8, "Chen Wu", "crew", 8),
    "dana@aurora.example": (9, "Dana Reyes", "crew", 9),
}
SLOT_NAMES = {1: "Flight Engineer", 2: "Mission Specialist", 3: "Commander"}


@dataclass
class Server:
    """Just enough of the API for S1 and S4."""

    api: FakeApi
    tokens: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    status: str = "draft"
    assignments: dict[int, dict] = field(default_factory=dict)
    approvals: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    history: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    accepted_reboost: set[int] = field(default_factory=set)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        on = self.api.on
        on("POST", "/auth/login", self.login)
        on("GET", "/me", self.authed(self.me))
        on("GET", "/skills", self.authed(lambda r, u: ok(SKILLS)))
        on("GET", "/crew", self.authed(lambda r, u: ok(CREW)))
        on("GET", "/users", self.authed(lambda r, u: ok(USERS)))
        on("GET", "/missions/1", self.authed(lambda r, u: ok(self.mission())))
        on("GET", "/missions/1/match", self.authed(self.match))
        on("POST", "/missions/1/match/apply", self.authed(self.apply))
        on("GET", "/assignments", self.authed(self.mine))
        for aid in (21, 22, 23):
            on("POST", f"/assignments/{aid}/accept", self.authed(self.accept(aid)))
        on("POST", "/missions/1/submit", self.authed(self.submit))
        on("POST", "/missions/1/approve", self.authed(self.approve))
        on("POST", "/missions/1/complete", self.authed(self.complete))
        on("GET", "/missions/1/history", self.authed(lambda r, u: ok(self.history)))

    # plumbing

    def authed(self, handler):
        def wrapped(request: httpx2.Request) -> httpx2.Response:
            auth = request.headers.get("authorization", "")
            email = self.tokens.get(auth.removeprefix("Bearer "))
            if email is None:
                return error(401, "unauthenticated", "Your session has ended")
            return handler(request, PEOPLE[email])

        return wrapped

    def login(self, request: httpx2.Request) -> httpx2.Response:
        body = json.loads(request.content)
        if body["email"] not in PEOPLE or body["password"] != "orbital launch window":
            return error(401, "invalid_credentials", "Email or password is incorrect")
        token = f"tok-{body['email'].split('@')[0]}"
        self.tokens[token] = body["email"]
        return ok({"token": token, "expires_at": "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z"})

    def me(self, request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
        uid, name, role, cpid = user
        email = next(e for e, p in PEOPLE.items() if p == user)
        return ok({"id": uid, "email": email, "name": name, "role": role, "active": True,
                   "crew_profile_id": cpid,
                   "organisation": {"id": 1, "name": "Aurora Space Agency"}})  # fmt: skip

    def log(self, user, event, from_status=None, to_status=None) -> None:
        self.history.append(
            {"id": len(self.history) + 1,
             "created_at": f"2027-01-05T09:{len(self.history):02d}:00Z",
             "actor_id": user[0], "actor_name": user[1], "event": event,
             "from_status": from_status, "to_status": to_status, "reason": None, "data": {}}
        )  # fmt: skip

    def mission(self) -> dict:
        open_places = {
            slot: 1 - sum(1 for a in self.assignments.values()
                          if a["slot_id"] == slot and a["status"] in ("proposed", "accepted"))
            for slot in SLOT_NAMES
        }  # fmt: skip
        m = gateway(self.status, list(self.assignments.values()), self.approvals, open_places)
        m["approvals_required"] = None if self.status == "draft" else 2
        return m

    # endpoints

    def match(self, request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
        from cli_fakes import S2_PREVIEW

        return ok(S2_PREVIEW | {"can_apply": user[0] == 4})

    def apply(self, request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
        for aid, slot, cpid, name in ((21, 3, 6, "Ana Okafor"), (22, 1, 8, "Chen Wu"),
                                      (23, 2, 9, "Dana Reyes")):  # fmt: skip
            self.assignments[aid] = {"id": aid, "slot_id": slot, "slot_name": SLOT_NAMES[slot],
                                     "crew_profile_id": cpid, "crew_name": name,
                                     "status": "proposed", "reason": None}  # fmt: skip
            self.log(user, "assignment.proposed")
        return ok(
            [{k: a[k] for k in ("id", "slot_id", "crew_profile_id", "status")}
             for a in self.assignments.values()],
            status=201,
        )  # fmt: skip

    def mine(self, request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
        rows = [
            {"assignment_id": a["id"], "mission_id": 1, "mission_name": "Lunar Gateway Resupply",
             "start_date": "2027-03-01", "end_date": "2027-03-10", "mission_status": self.status,
             "slot_name": a["slot_name"], "status": a["status"], "reason": None}
            for a in self.assignments.values() if a["crew_profile_id"] == user[3]
        ]  # fmt: skip
        if request.url.params.get("pending") == "true":
            rows = [r for r in rows if r["status"] == "proposed"]
        return ok(rows)

    def accept(self, aid):
        def handler(request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
            a = self.assignments.get(aid)
            if a is None or a["crew_profile_id"] != user[3]:
                return error(404, "not_found", "Assignment not found")
            a["status"] = "accepted"
            self.log(user, "assignment.accepted", "proposed", "accepted")
```

            return ok({"id": aid, "mission_id": 1, "slot_id": a["slot_id"],
                       "crew_profile_id": a["crew_profile_id"], "status": "accepted",
                       "reason": None})  # fmt: skip

        return handler

    def submit(self, request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
        waiting = [a for a in self.assignments.values() if a["status"] != "accepted"]
        if waiting:
            return error(409, "rule_violation", "Mission can't be submitted", [
                problem("proposal_unanswered",
                        f"{a['slot_name']}: {a['crew_name']} hasn't responded to the proposal",
                        assignment_id=a["id"]) for a in waiting
            ])  # fmt: skip
        self.status = "pending_approval"
        self.log(user, "mission.submitted", "draft", "pending_approval")
        return ok(self.mission())

    def approve(self, request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
        if user[0] == 1:
            return error(403, "approver_is_creator", "You can't approve a mission you created")
        self.approvals.append({"director_id": user[0], "director_name": user[1],
                               "decision": "approve", "reason": None})  # fmt: skip
        self.log(user, "mission.approved")
        if len(self.approvals) == 2:
            self.status = "active"
            self.log(user, "mission.activated", "pending_approval", "active")
        return ok(self.mission())

    def complete(self, request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
        self.status = "completed"
        for a in self.assignments.values():
            a["status"] = "completed"
        self.log(user, "mission.completed", "active", "completed")
        return ok(self.mission())


def _login(mc, email):
    result = mc("login", "--email", email, input="orbital launch window\n")
    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.stderr
    return result


def test_s1_golden_path_through_the_cli(mc, api):
    server = Server(api)

    _login(mc, "lee@aurora.example")
    preview = mc("missions", "match", "1")
    assert preview.exit_code == 0
    assert "Commander            Ana" in preview.stdout
    applied = mc("missions", "match", "1", "--apply")
    assert applied.exit_code == 0, applied.stderr
    assert "Proposed 3 crew for Lunar Gateway Resupply" in applied.stdout

    early = mc("missions", "submit", "1")
    assert early.exit_code == 1  # AC-2: proposals still unanswered
    assert "  • Commander: Ana Okafor hasn't responded to the proposal\n" in early.stderr

    for email, aid in (("ana", 21), ("chen", 22), ("dana", 23)):
        _login(mc, f"{email}@aurora.example")
        pending = mc("assignments", "list", "--pending", "--json")
        assert [row["assignment_id"] for row in json.loads(pending.stdout)] == [aid]
        accepted = mc("assignments", "accept", str(aid))
        assert accepted.exit_code == 0, accepted.stderr
        assert accepted.stdout.startswith("✓ Accepted ")

    _login(mc, "lee@aurora.example")
    submitted = mc("missions", "submit", "1")
    assert submitted.stdout == (
        "✓ Submitted Lunar Gateway Resupply for approval. It needs 2 approvals.\n"
    )

    _login(mc, "ruth@aurora.example")
    ruth = mc("missions", "approve", "1")
    assert ruth.exit_code == 1  # AC-8: the creator can't approve
    assert ruth.stderr == "✗ You can't approve a mission you created.\n"

    _login(mc, "marco@aurora.example")
    marco = mc("missions", "approve", "1")
    assert marco.stdout == (
        "✓ Approved Lunar Gateway Resupply. It's pending approval (1 of 2 approvals).\n"
    )

    _login(mc, "ingrid@aurora.example")
    ingrid = mc("missions", "approve", "1")
    assert ingrid.stdout == "✓ Approved Lunar Gateway Resupply. It's now active.\n"

    _login(mc, "lee@aurora.example")
    completed = mc("missions", "complete", "1")
    assert completed.exit_code == 0

    shown = json.loads(mc("missions", "show", "1", "--json").stdout)
    assert shown["status"] == "completed"
    assert {a["status"] for a in shown["assignments"]} == {"completed"}

    history = mc("missions", "history", "1").stdout.splitlines()  # AC-1: who did what, when
    for who, what in (
        ("Lee Park", "Submitted for approval"),
        ("Marco Bianchi", "Approved"),
        ("Ingrid Holm", "Mission became active"),
        ("Lee Park", "Completed"),
    ):
        assert any(who in line and what in line and "Jan 2027" in line for line in history)
    assert server.status == "completed"


def test_s4_overlaps_and_unavailability_through_the_cli(mc, api):
    tokens = {"tok-lee": "lee", "tok-nora": "nora", "tok-chen": "chen"}
    proposals: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []

    def who(request):
        return tokens[request.headers["authorization"].removeprefix("Bearer ")]

    reboost = gateway() | {
        "id": 3,
        "name": "Station Reboost",
        "start_date": "2027-02-25",
        "end_date": "2027-03-02",
        "owner_id": 5,
        "owner_name": "Nora Quinn",
    }
    reboost["slots"] = [{"id": 6, "position": 1, "name": "Engineer", "headcount": 2,
                         "open_places": 1, "requirements": []}]  # fmt: skip
    api.on("GET", "/skills", ok(SKILLS))
    api.on("GET", "/crew", ok(CREW))
    api.on("GET", "/missions/1", ok(gateway()))
    api.on("GET", "/missions/3", ok(reboost))

    def propose_gateway(request):
        body = json.loads(request.content)
        if body["crew_profile_id"] == 12:
            return error(409, "rule_violation", "Can't propose Gus Patel for Flight Engineer", [
                problem("overlap", "Gus Patel has an accepted assignment on Station Reboost "
                        "overlapping 1–2 Mar", crew_profile_id=12, mission_id=3,
                        mission_name="Station Reboost")])  # fmt: skip
        if body["crew_profile_id"] == 11:
            return error(409, "rule_violation", "Can't propose Fay Moreau for Commander", [
                problem("unavailable", "Fay Moreau is unavailable 5–8 Mar",
                        crew_profile_id=11, slot_id=3)])  # fmt: skip
        proposals.append((who(request), body))
        return ok({"id": 25, "mission_id": 1, "slot_id": 1, "crew_profile_id": 8,
                   "status": "proposed", "reason": None}, status=201)  # fmt: skip

    def propose_reboost(request):
        proposals.append((who(request), json.loads(request.content)))
        return ok({"id": 24, "mission_id": 3, "slot_id": 6, "crew_profile_id": 8,
                   "status": "proposed", "reason": None}, status=201)  # fmt: skip

    api.on("POST", "/missions/1/assignments", propose_gateway)
    api.on("POST", "/missions/3/assignments", propose_reboost)
    api.on("GET", "/assignments", ok([]))
    api.on("POST", "/assignments/24/accept", ok({"id": 24, "status": "accepted"}))
    api.on(
        "POST",
        "/assignments/25/accept",
        error(409, "overlap", "You have an accepted assignment on Station Reboost overlapping "
              "1–2 Mar"),
    )  # fmt: skip

    mc.login_as("tok-lee")
    gus = mc(
        "missions", "propose", "1", "--slot", "Flight Engineer", "--crew", "gus@aurora.example"
    )
    fay = mc("missions", "propose", "1", "--slot", "Commander", "--crew", "fay@aurora.example")

    assert gus.exit_code == fay.exit_code == 1  # AC-14
    assert gus.stderr == (
        "✗ Can't propose Gus Patel for Flight Engineer.\n"
        "  • Gus Patel has an accepted assignment on Station Reboost overlapping 1–2 Mar\n"
        "  See who is free: mc missions match 1\n"
    )
    assert "  • Fay Moreau is unavailable 5–8 Mar\n" in fay.stderr

    mc.login_as("tok-nora", "nora@aurora.example")
    assert mc("missions", "propose", "3", "--slot", "Engineer", "--crew",
              "chen@aurora.example").exit_code == 0  # fmt: skip
    mc.login_as("tok-lee")
    assert mc("missions", "propose", "1", "--slot", "1", "--crew",
              "chen@aurora.example").exit_code == 0  # fmt: skip
    assert proposals == [
        ("nora", {"slot_id": 6, "crew_profile_id": 8}),
        ("lee", {"slot_id": 1, "crew_profile_id": 8}),
    ]

    mc.login_as("tok-chen", "chen@aurora.example")
    first = mc("assignments", "accept", "24")
    second = mc("assignments", "accept", "25")

    assert first.exit_code == 0
    assert second.exit_code == 1  # AC-15: names the conflicting mission
    assert second.stderr.startswith(
        "✗ You have an accepted assignment on Station Reboost overlapping 1–2 Mar.\n"
    )

<summary>↳ Result</summary>

```
"""Turning refusals into HTTP responses. Every error, whatever its status, has one shape:

    {"error": {"code": ..., "message": ..., "problems": [{"code", "message", "refs"}, ...]}}

`problems` always has at least one entry. A refusal with a single reason repeats its message
there, so clients only ever read one format.
"""

from collections.abc import Sequence
from http import HTTPStatus
from typing import Any

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from starlette.exceptions import HTTPException as StarletteHTTPException

from mission_control.domain.dates import InvalidDateRange
from mission_control.domain.errors import (
    Forbidden,
    InvalidInput,
    NotFound,
    Problem,
    RuleViolation,
    Unauthenticated,
)
from mission_control.domain.passwords import MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH, PasswordTooShort
from mission_control.infra.db import DatabaseBusy

VALIDATION_ERROR = "validation_error"
_SEVERAL_MISTAKES = "Some values aren't valid"


def error_response(
    status: int,
    code: str,
    message: str,
    problems: Sequence[Problem] = (),
    headers: dict[str, str] | None = None,
) -> JSONResponse:
    listed = problems or (Problem(code, message),)
    body = {
        "error": {
            "code": code,
            "message": message,
            "problems": [
                {"code": p.code, "message": p.message, "refs": dict(p.refs)} for p in listed
            ],
        }
    }
    return JSONResponse(body, status_code=status, headers=headers)


def install(app: FastAPI) -> None:
    """Register a handler for every refusal the services raise, and for malformed requests."""

    @app.exception_handler(Unauthenticated)
    def _unauthenticated(_: Request, e: Unauthenticated) -> JSONResponse:
        return error_response(401, e.code, e.message, headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"})

    @app.exception_handler(Forbidden)
    def _forbidden(_: Request, e: Forbidden) -> JSONResponse:
        return error_response(403, e.code, e.message)

    @app.exception_handler(NotFound)
    def _not_found(_: Request, e: NotFound) -> JSONResponse:
        return error_response(404, e.code, e.message)

    @app.exception_handler(RuleViolation)  # StateConflict included
    def _rule_violation(_: Request, e: RuleViolation) -> JSONResponse:
        return error_response(409, e.code, e.message, e.problems)

    @app.exception_handler(InvalidInput)
    def _invalid_input(_: Request, e: InvalidInput) -> JSONResponse:
        return error_response(422, VALIDATION_ERROR, e.message, e.problems)

    @app.exception_handler(PasswordTooShort)
    def _password_too_short(_: Request, e: PasswordTooShort) -> JSONResponse:
        message = f"Passwords must be at least {MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH} characters"
        return error_response(422, "password_too_short", message)
2:from dataclasses import dataclass, field
6:@dataclass(frozen=True)
7:class Problem:
11:    code: str
19:class Forbidden(Exception):
22:    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
24:        self.code = code
28:class Unauthenticated(Exception):
31:    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
33:        self.code = code
37:class NotFound(Exception):
41:    code = "not_found"
48:class RuleViolation(Exception):
51:    A rule refusal's code is always `rule_violation`, with a summary message; each reason is a
52:    problem with its own code. Only state conflicts (`StateConflict`) set a code of their own."""
54:    def __init__(self, message: str, problems: Sequence[Problem], code: str = "rule_violation"):
58:        self.code = code
62:    @classmethod
64:        """A refusal with a single reason, which also serves as its message. The code stays
65:        `rule_violation`; the reason's own code is in `problems`."""
69:class InvalidInput(ValueError):
75:    code = "validation_error"
80:        self.problems = tuple(problems) or (Problem(self.code, message),)

def session(token: str, me: Mapping[str, Any], expires_at: str = "2027-01-05T21:00:00Z") -> dict:

    user = {k: me[k] for k in ("id", "email", "name", "role", "active")}

"""M16: signing in and out, who am I, and changing a password (PRD §4.1, P10; design §8).

Passwords are only ever asked for with a hidden prompt, never taken as a flag. When stdin isn't a
terminal they're read from it, one line per prompt, so scripts can pipe them in.
"""

import stat
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta

from cli_fakes import LEE_ME, error, ok


def _login_ok(request):
    expires = datetime.now(UTC) + timedelta(hours=12)
    return ok({"token": "tok-new", "expires_at": expires.isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")})


def test_m16_login_prompts_for_email_and_hidden_password_then_stores_the_token(mc, api):
    api.on("POST", "/auth/login", _login_ok)
    api.on("GET", "/me", ok(LEE_ME))

def test_v2_empty_mission_list(mc, api):
    mc.login_as("tok-ben", "ben@aurora.example")
    api.on("GET", "/missions", ok([]))

    result = mc("missions", "list")

    assert result.exit_code == 0
    assert result.stdout == "No missions to show.\n"


def test_m16_show_mission_lists_slots_crew_and_approvals(mc, api):
    mc.login_as()
    crew_rows = [
        {"id": 21, "slot_id": 1, "slot_name": "Flight Engineer", "crew_profile_id": 8,
         "crew_name": "Chen Wu", "status": "accepted", "reason": None},
    ]  # fmt: skip
    approvals = [
        {"director_id": 2, "director_name": "Marco Bianchi", "decision": "approve", "reason": None}
    ]
    api.on(
        "GET",
        "/missions/1",
        ok(gateway("pending_approval", crew_rows, approvals, open_places={1: 0})),
    )

    result = mc("missions", "show", "1")

    assert result.exit_code == 0
    assert result.stdout == (
        "Lunar Gateway Resupply · 1–10 Mar 2027 · Pending approval\n"
        "Owner Lee Park · created by Ruth Adeyemi\n"
        "\n"
        "  #   Slot                 Requires                     Places\n"
        "  1   Flight Engineer      Systems Engineering>=3       full\n"
        "  2   Mission Specialist   EVA>=3                       1 of 1 open\n"
        "  3   Commander            Piloting>=4, Leadership>=3   1 of 1 open\n"
        "\n"
        "  Assignment   Crew      Slot              Status\n"
        "  21           Chen Wu   Flight Engineer   accepted\n"
        "\n"
        "Approvals: 1 of 2 (Marco Bianchi)\n"
    )


def test_v1_history_shows_who_did_what_and_when(mc, api):
    mc.login_as()
    api.on(
        "GET",
        "/missions/6/history",
        ok(
            [
                {"id": 1, "created_at": "2027-01-02T10:00:00.000Z", "actor_id": 5,
                 "actor_name": "Nora Quinn", "event": "mission.submitted",
                 "from_status": "draft", "to_status": "pending_approval", "reason": None,
                 "data": {}},
                {"id": 2, "created_at": "2027-01-03T11:30:00.000Z", "actor_id": 7,
                 "actor_name": "Ben Ito", "event": "assignment.withdrawn",
                 "from_status": "accepted", "to_status": "withdrawn", "reason": "Family matter",
                 "data": {"slot_name": "Engineer", "crew_name": "Ben Ito"}},
            ]
        ),
    )  # fmt: skip

    result = mc("missions", "history", "6")

    assert result.exit_code == 0
    assert result.stdout == (
        "  When (UTC)         Who          What                     Reason\n"
        "  2 Jan 2027 10:00   Nora Quinn   Submitted for approval\n"
        "  3 Jan 2027 11:30   Ben Ito      Withdrew from Engineer   Family matter\n"
    )


# Confirmations (C4)


def test_c4_editing_a_pending_mission_asks_first_and_yes_changes_it(mc, api):
    mc.login_as()
    api.on("GET", "/missions/1", ok(gateway("pending_approval")))
    api.on("PATCH", "/missions/1", ok(gateway()))

def test_m16_remove_crew_checks_the_assignment_is_on_that_mission(mc, api):
    mc.login_as()
    api.on("GET", "/missions/1", ok(gateway()))

    result = mc("missions", "remove-crew", "1", "99")

    assert result.exit_code == 1
    assert result.stderr == (
        "✗ Assignment 99 isn't on Lunar Gateway Resupply.\n  See its crew: mc missions show 1\n"
    )
    assert api.writes() == []


def test_ac4_remove_crew_from_a_pending_mission_confirms(mc, api):
    mc.login_as()
    row = {"id": 21, "slot_id": 1, "slot_name": "Flight Engineer", "crew_profile_id": 8,
           "crew_name": "Chen Wu", "status": "accepted", "reason": None}  # fmt: skip
    api.on("GET", "/missions/1", ok(gateway("pending_approval", [row])))
    api.on("POST", "/assignments/21/remove", ok(row | {"status": "removed"}))

    result = mc("missions", "remove-crew", "1", "21", "--yes")

    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.stderr
    assert result.stdout == (
        "✓ Removed Chen Wu from Flight Engineer on Lunar Gateway Resupply. It's back in Draft.\n"
    )

        "new_password": "new passphrase is long",
    }

    )
    assert "Next: mc login" in result.stderr
    assert not mc.store.path.exists()

    moment = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00")).astimezone(UTC)
    return f"{moment.day} {moment.strftime('%b %Y %H:%M')}"


# Tables


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Span:
    """A row whose first cell lines up with the column and whose text runs across the rest."""

    first: str
    rest: str


def table_lines(
    headers: Sequence[str], rows: Iterable[Sequence[str] | Span], indent: str = _INDENT
) -> list[str]:
    """Columns padded by display width, three spaces apart, with no trailing spaces."""
    rows = list(rows)
    widths = [cell_len(h) for h in headers]
    for row in rows:
        cells = [row.first] if isinstance(row, Span) else row
        for i, cell in enumerate(cells):
            widths[i] = max(widths[i], cell_len(cell))

    def line(cells: Sequence[str]) -> str:
        padded = [c + " " * (widths[i] - cell_len(c)) for i, c in enumerate(cells)]
        return (indent + _GAP.join(padded)).rstrip()

    lines = [line(headers)]
    for row in rows:
        if isinstance(row, Span):
            pad = " " * (widths[0] - cell_len(row.first))
            lines.append((indent + row.first + pad + _GAP + row.rest).rstrip())
        else:
            lines.append(line(row))
    return lines


def field_lines(pairs: Sequence[tuple[str, str]]) -> list[str]:
    width = max(cell_len(k) for k, _ in pairs)
    return [f"{k}{' ' * (width - cell_len(k))}{_GAP}{v}".rstrip() for k, v in pairs]


# Words


def join_names(names: Sequence[str]) -> str:
    """`Ana`, `Ana and Chen`, `Ana, Chen and Dana`."""
    if len(names) <= 1:
        return "".join(names)
    return f"{', '.join(names[:-1])} and {names[-1]}"


def plural(count: int, word: str, many: str | None = None) -> str:
    return f"{count} {word if count == 1 else many or word + 's'}"


def sentence(text: str) -> str:
    """End a server message with a full stop unless it already has one."""
    text = text.strip()
    return text if not text or text[-1] in ".!?" else text + "."


class Terminal:
    """Writes data to stdout and messages to stderr, each styled for its own stream."""

    def __init__(self, out: TextIO, err: TextIO, out_style: Style, err_style: Style) -> None:
        self.out = out
        self.err = err
        self.out_style = out_style
        self.err_style = err_style

    @classmethod
    def for_streams(cls, out: TextIO, err: TextIO, color: bool | None = None) -> "Terminal":
        out_style, err_style = detect_style(out, os.environ), detect_style(err, os.environ)
        if color is not None:
            out_style = Style(color, out_style.unicode)
            err_style = Style(color, err_style.unicode)
        return cls(out, err, out_style, err_style)

    # Plain-text forms of the separators

    @property
    def sep(self) -> str:
        return " · " if self.out_style.unicode else " - "

    def dates(self, start: str, end: str) -> str:
        return format_range(parse_date(start), parse_date(end), plain=not self.out_style.unicode)

    def heading(self, *parts: str) -> str:
        return self.sep.join(p for p in parts if p)

    # stdout

    def line(self, text: str = "") -> None:
        self.out.write(text + "\n")

    def lines(self, lines: Iterable[str]) -> None:
        for text in lines:
            self.line(text)

    def table(self, headers: Sequence[str], rows: Iterable[Sequence[str] | Span]) -> None:
        self.lines(table_lines(headers, rows))

    def fields(self, pairs: Sequence[tuple[str, str]]) -> None:
        self.lines(field_lines(pairs))

    def json(self, data: Any) -> None:
        self.line(json.dumps(data, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))

    def ok(self, message: str) -> None:
        self.line(self._marker("✓", "OK:", _GREEN, self.out_style) + " " + message)

    def note(self, message: str) -> None:
        self.line(self._marker("!", "Note:", _YELLOW, self.out_style) + " " + message)

    # stderr

    def say(self, text: str = "") -> None:
        self.out.flush()  # keep data and messages in order when stdout is a pipe
        self.err.write(text + "\n")
        self.err.flush()

    def warn(self, message: str) -> None:
        self.say(self._marker("!", "Note:", _YELLOW, self.err_style) + " " + message)

    def error(
        self, message: str, bullets: Sequence[str] = (), hint: str | Sequence[str] | None = None
    ) -> None:
        self.say(self._marker("✗", "Error:", _RED, self.err_style) + " " + message)
        bullet = "•" if self.err_style.unicode else "-"
        for text in bullets:
            self.say(f"{_INDENT}{bullet} {text}")
        hints = [hint] if isinstance(hint, str) else list(hint or ())
        for text in hints:
            self.say(_INDENT + text)

    def next_steps(self, steps: Sequence[str]) -> None:
        for i, step in enumerate(steps):
            self.say(("Next: " if i == 0 else "      ") + step)

    @staticmethod
    def _marker(symbol: str, word: str, colour: str, style: Style) -> str:
        if not style.unicode:
            return word
        return f"{colour}{symbol}{_RESET}" if style.color else symbol

def test_m16_unreachable_server_exits_4_and_says_how_to_start_it(mc):
    mc.login_as()

    def refuse(request):
        raise httpx2.ConnectError("connection refused", request=request)

    result = mc("whoami", http=httpx2.Client(transport=httpx2.MockTransport(refuse)))

    assert result.exit_code == 4
    assert "✗ Can't reach the Mission Control server at " in result.stderr
    assert "Is it running? Start it with: uv run mc-server" in result.stderr
    assert mc.store.path.exists()


def test_m16_busy_database_exits_4_with_a_retry_hint(mc, api):
    mc.login_as()
    api.on("GET", "/me", error(503, "busy", "The database is busy"))
```

    assert result.exit_code == 1
    assert "pending approval" not in result.stderr
    assert result.stderr.startswith("✗ Only the mission's owner can edit this mission.\\n")


def test_c4_a_director_editing_is_not_asked_either(mc, api):
    mc.login_as("tok-ruth", "ruth@aurora.example")
    api.on("GET", "/me", ok(RUTH_ME))
    api.on("GET", "/missions/1", ok(gateway("pending_approval")))
    api.on("PATCH", "/missions/1", error(403, "role_forbidden", "Directors can't edit missions"))

    result = mc("missions", "edit", "1", "--name", "New", answers=[])

    assert result.exit_code == 1
    assert result.asked == []
    assert result.stderr == "✗ Directors can't edit missions.\\n"


def test_c4_transferring_a_pending_mission_asks_a_director(mc, api):
    mc.login_as("tok-ruth", "ruth@aurora.example")
    api.on("GET", "/me", ok(RUTH_ME))
    api.on("GET", "/users", ok(USERS))
    api.on("GET", "/missions/1", ok(gateway("pending_approval")))

    result = mc("missions", "transfer", "1", "--owner", "nora@aurora.example", answers=["n"])

    assert result.exit_code == 1
    assert result.asked == [("Continue? [y/N]", False)]
    assert api.writes() == []


def test_c4_no_confirmation_for_a_transfer_by_a_lead(mc, api):
    mc.login_as()
    api.on("GET", "/me", ok(LEE_ME))
    api.on("GET", "/users", ok(USERS))
    api.on("GET", "/missions/1", ok(gateway("pending_approval")))
    api.on(
        "POST",
        "/missions/1/owner",
        error(403, "role_forbidden", "Leads can't transfer mission ownership"),
    )

    result = mc("missions", "transfer", "1", "--owner", "nora@aurora.example", answers=[])

    assert result.exit_code == 1
    assert result.asked == []
    assert result.stderr == "✗ Leads can't transfer mission ownership.\\n"


def test_ac6_cancel_needs_a_reason_flag(mc, api):''')

edit('commands/account.py','''def logout(ctx: typer.Context) -> None:
    """Log out and end this session on the server."""
    env = start(ctx)
    if env.store.load() is None:
        env.term.ok("You're not logged in.")
        return
    try:
        env.api().post("/auth/logout")
    except ApiError as error:

    env.store.clear()

    """Logging in again replaces the saved session, so end the old one on the server rather than
    leave it valid until it expires. Best effort: the new login has already worked."""
    if env.store.load() is None:
        return
    try:
        env.api().post("/auth/logout")
    except (ApiError, Unreachable):
        pass


def logout(ctx: typer.Context) -> None:
    """Log out and end this session on the server.

    If the server can't be reached, the session is still removed from this computer.
    """
    env = start(ctx)
    if env.store.load() is None:
        env.term.ok("You're not logged in.")
        return
    try:
        env.api().post("/auth/logout")
    except ApiError as error:
        if error.status != 401:  # a session that already ended is as good as logged out
            raise
    except Unreachable:
        env.store.clear()
        env.term.warn(
            "Couldn't reach the server, so the session may stay valid until it expires. "
            "Removed it from this computer."
        )
        raise typer.Exit(UNAVAILABLE) from None
    env.store.clear()
    env.term.ok("Logged out.")''')

    return me["id"] == mission["owner_id"]


def _is_director(me: dict[str, Any], mission: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    return me["role"] == "director"


def _is_owner_or_director(me: dict[str, Any], mission: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    return _is_owner(me, mission) or _is_director(me, mission)


Allowed = Callable[[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]], bool]


def _confirm(
    env: Env,
    api: Api,
    mission: dict[str, Any],
    warning: str,
    yes: bool,
    allowed: Allowed,
    question: str = "Continue?",
) -> None:
    """Ask before a change that can't easily be undone, but only someone the server will let make
    it: anyone else just sends the request and sees the server's refusal."""
    if yes or not allowed(api.get("/me"), mission):
        return
    confirm(env, warning, yes=False, question=question)


def _confirm_if_pending(
    env: Env,
    api: Api,
    mission: dict[str, Any],
    doing: str,
    yes: bool,
    allowed: Allowed = _is_owner,
) -> bool:
    """Ask before a change that sends a pending mission back to Draft. True if it was pending."""
    if mission["status"] != "pending_approval":
        return False
    warning = (
        f"{mission['name']} is pending approval. {doing} sends it back to Draft and clears its "
        "approvals."
    )
    _confirm(env, api, mission, warning, yes, allowed)
    return True''')

"""Checks a scripted response against the real API, so the CLI's test doubles can't drift from it.

Every route is read from the API's routers: its method, path, success status and response
model (`test_contract_covers_every_api_route` keeps that list in step with the app). A fake response must be for a route that exists, use that route's success status, and
have a body the response model accepts with no fields the model doesn't have. Error responses
must have the design's error shape. A plain 500 is allowed, as the real server sends one for
an unhandled fault.
"""

import json
        "/missions/6/history",
        ok(
            [
                {"id": 1, "created_at": "2027-01-02T10:00:00.000Z", "actor_id": 5,
                 "actor_name": "Nora Quinn", "event": "mission.submitted",
                 "assignment_id": None, "crew_profile_id": None, "from_status": "draft", "to_status": "pending_approval", "reason": None,
                 "data": {}},
                {"id": 2, "created_at": "2027-01-03T11:30:00.000Z", "actor_id": 7,
                 "actor_name": "Ben Ito", "event": "assignment.withdrawn",
                 "assignment_id": 31, "crew_profile_id": 7, "from_status": "accepted", "to_status": "withdrawn", "reason": "Family matter",
                 "data": {"slot_name": "Engineer", "crew_name": "Ben Ito"}},
            ]
        ),

    def log(self, user, event, from_status=None, to_status=None) -> None:
        self.history.append(
            {"id": len(self.history) + 1,
             "created_at": f"2027-01-05T09:{len(self.history):02d}:00Z",
             "actor_id": user[0], "actor_name": user[1], "event": event,
             "assignment_id": None, "crew_profile_id": None, "from_status": from_status, "to_status": to_status, "reason": None, "data": {}}
        )  # fmt: skip
```

"""Checks a scripted response against the real API, so the CLI's test doubles can't drift from it.

Every route is read from the API's routers: its method, path, success status and response
model (`test_contract_covers_every_api_route` keeps that list in step with the app).

- A fake response must be for a route that exists and use that route's success status.
- Its body must be one the response model accepts, with no fields the model doesn't have.
- An error must have the design's error shape, with a code its status can carry.
- A plain 500 is allowed, as the real server sends one for an unhandled fault.
"""

import json
from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any

import httpx2
from fastapi.routing import APIRoute
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, TypeAdapter, ValidationError

from mission_control.api.routers import admin, assignments, auth, crew, missions
from mission_control.api.schemas.missions import MissionSummaryOut

# Fields the CLI already reads that the API is about to add. Each is allowed only while the model
# lacks it; once the model has it, it's checked like any other field.
_COMING: Mapping[type[BaseModel], frozenset[str]] = {MissionSummaryOut: frozenset({"owner_name"})}

ROUTES = [
    r
    for module in (auth, admin, crew, missions, assignments)
    for r in module.router.routes
    if isinstance(r, APIRoute)
]


# The top-level error codes each status may carry (design §7, Errors). A rule refusal's reasons are
# problem codes inside `problems`, never the top-level code.
_ERROR_CODES: Mapping[int, frozenset[str]] = {
    401: frozenset({"invalid_credentials", "unauthenticated"}),
    403: frozenset(
        {
            "role_forbidden",
            "not_owner",
            "approver_is_creator",
            "approver_is_rostered",
            "self_skill_edit",
            "wrong_current_password",
        }
    ),
    404: frozenset({"not_found"}),
    409: frozenset(
        {
            "mission_frozen",
            "mission_final",
            "invalid_transition",
            "already_decided",
            "rule_violation",
        }
    ),
    422: frozenset({"validation_error", "password_too_short"}),
    503: frozenset({"busy"}),
}


class _Strict(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")


class _ProblemBody(_Strict):
    code: str
    message: str
    refs: dict[str, Any]


class _ErrorDetail(_Strict):
    code: str
    message: str
    problems: list[_ProblemBody]


class _ErrorBody(_Strict):
    error: _ErrorDetail


def _route(method: str, path: str) -> APIRoute | None:
    for route in ROUTES:
        if method in route.methods and route.path_regex.match(path):
            return route
    return None


def problems(method: str, path: str, response: httpx2.Response) -> list[str]:
    """What's wrong with a scripted response to `method path` (path without the /api/v1 prefix).
    Empty when it's a response the real API could send."""
    where = f"{method} {path}"
    route = _route(method, path)
    if route is None:
        return [f"{where}: the API has no such endpoint"]
    status = response.status_code
    if status == 500:
        return []  # an unhandled server fault: the real server sends plain text too
    try:
        body = json.loads(response.content) if response.content else None
    except ValueError:
        return [f"{where}: the body isn't JSON"]

    if status >= 400:
        try:
            _ErrorBody.model_validate(body)
        except ValidationError as exc:
            return [f"{where}: error body doesn't match the error shape: {exc}"]
        error = body["error"]
        if not error["problems"]:
            return [f"{where}: an error lists at least one problem"]
        if error["code"] not in _ERROR_CODES.get(status, ()):
            return [f"{where}: the API doesn't send {error['code']!r} with a {status}"]
        return []

    expected = route.status_code or 200
    if status != expected:
        return [f"{where}: answers {expected}, not {status}"]
    if expected == 204:
        return [] if body is None else [f"{where}: a 204 has no body"]

    adapter: TypeAdapter[Any] = TypeAdapter(route.response_model)
    try:
        parsed = adapter.validate_python(body)
    except ValidationError as exc:
        return [f"{where}: body doesn't match {_name(route)}: {exc}"]
    return [f"{where}: {p}" for p in _unknown(body, parsed, "body")]


def _name(route: APIRoute) -> str:
    model = route.response_model
    return getattr(model, "__name__", None) or str(model)


def _unknown(sent: Any, parsed: Any, at: str) -> list[str]:
    """Fields in `sent` that the parsed model doesn't have, anywhere in the tree."""
    found: list[str] = []
    if isinstance(parsed, BaseModel) and isinstance(sent, dict):
        fields = type(parsed).model_fields
        allowed = set(fields) | _coming(type(parsed))
        for key, value in sent.items():
            if key not in allowed:
                found.append(f"{at}.{key} isn't in {type(parsed).__name__}")
            elif key in fields:
                found += _unknown(value, getattr(parsed, key), f"{at}.{key}")
    elif isinstance(parsed, list | tuple) and isinstance(sent, list):
        for i, (s, p) in enumerate(zip(sent, parsed, strict=False)):
            found += _unknown(s, p, f"{at}[{i}]")
    elif isinstance(parsed, Mapping) and isinstance(sent, dict):
        for key, value in sent.items():
            found += _unknown(value, parsed.get(key), f"{at}.{key}")
    return found


def _coming(model: type[BaseModel]) -> frozenset[str]:
    names: set[str] = set()
    for base, extra in _COMING.items():
        if issubclass(model, base):
            names |= extra - set(model.model_fields)
    return frozenset(names)
```

</details>

```bash
cat > /tmp/fixcli/pty_run.py <<'EOF'
"""Run a command on a pseudo-terminal, answer its prompt, and print the transcript."""
import os, pty, sys, time, select

answer, argv = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2:]
pid, fd = pty.fork()
if pid == 0:
    os.execvp(argv[0], argv)
out = b""
sent = False
while True:
    r, _, _ = select.select([fd], [], [], 5)
    if not r:
        break
    try:
        chunk = os.read(fd, 4096)
    except OSError:
        break
    if not chunk:
        break
    out += chunk
    if not sent and b"[y/N]" in out:
        os.write(fd, answer.encode() + b"\n")
        sent = True
_, status = os.waitpid(pid, 0)
sys.stdout.write(out.decode().replace("\r\n", "\n"))
print(f"exit {os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status)}")
EOF
M=/tmp/fixcli/mc.sh; echo '$ mc users deactivate zed@aurora.example   # Ruth, terminal, answers n'; python3 /tmp/fixcli/pty_run.py n $M ruth users deactivate zed@aurora.example; echo; echo '$ mc users deactivate zed@aurora.example   # Ruth, terminal, answers y'; python3 /tmp/fixcli/pty_run.py y $M ruth users deactivate zed@aurora.example; echo; echo '$ mc users deactivate chen@aurora.example --yes < /dev/null   # Ruth'; $M ruth users deactivate chen@aurora.example --yes < /dev/null; echo "exit $?"
```
